51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,

54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 457-477
Documentation:

Method code:
457 |     @Override
458 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
459 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
460 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
461 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
462 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());
463 | 
464 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> predNode : predNodes) {
465 |         BranchedFlowState<L> predNodeState = predNode.getAnnotation();
466 | 
467 |         L in = predNodeState.out.get(
468 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
469 | 
470 |         values.add(in);
471 |       }
472 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
473 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
474 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
475 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
476 |       }
477 |     }

===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1253-1320
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1253 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1254 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1255 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1256 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1257 |       if (relationship != null) {
1258 |         FunctionType superCtor = getFunctionType(
1259 |             scope.getVar(relationship.superclassName));
1260 |         FunctionType subCtor = getFunctionType(
1261 |             scope.getVar(relationship.subclassName));
1262 |         if (superCtor != null && superCtor.isConstructor() &&
1263 |             subCtor != null && subCtor.isConstructor()) {
1264 |           ObjectType superClass = superCtor.getInstanceType();
1265 |           ObjectType subClass = subCtor.getInstanceType();
1266 | 
1267 |           // superCtor and subCtor might be structural constructors
1268 |           // (like {function(new:Object)}) so we need to resolve them back
1269 |           // to the original ctor objects.
1270 |           superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1271 |           subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1272 | 
1273 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS &&
1274 |               !superClass.isEmptyType() && !subClass.isEmptyType()) {
1275 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1276 |           }
1277 | 
1278 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1279 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1280 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1281 |           }
1282 |         }
1283 |       }
1284 | 
1285 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1286 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1287 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1288 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1289 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1290 |         if (objectType != null) {
1291 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1292 | 
1293 |           if (functionType != null) {
1294 |             FunctionType getterType =
1295 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1296 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1297 |                 objectType);
1298 |           }
1299 |         }
1300 |       }
1301 | 
1302 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1303 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1304 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1305 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1306 |       }
1307 | 
1308 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1309 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1310 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1311 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1312 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1313 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1314 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1315 |         } else {
1316 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1317 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1318 |         }
1319 |       }
1320 |     }

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
85 |     try {
86 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
87 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
88 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
89 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
90 |           logger_);
91 |     } catch (IOException e) {
92 |       compiler.report(
93 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.SCRIPT);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
106 |   }

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 167-188
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
167 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
168 |     if (isAllType) {
169 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
170 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
171 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
172 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
173 |       } else {
174 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
175 |       }
176 |     } else {
177 |       int size = alternates.size();
178 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
179 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
180 |       } else if (size > 1) {
181 |         return null;
182 |       } else if (size == 1) {
183 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
184 |       } else {
185 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
186 |       }
187 |     }
188 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 118-154
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
118 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
119 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
120 |                 Scope functionScope,
121 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
122 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
123 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
124 |     this.compiler = compiler;
125 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
126 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
127 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
128 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
129 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
130 | 
131 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
132 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
133 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
134 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
139 |     // type is VOID.
140 |     Iterator<Var> varIt =
141 |         functionScope.getDeclarativelyUnboundVarsWithoutTypes();
142 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
143 |       Var var = varIt.next();
144 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
145 |         continue;
146 |       }
147 | 
148 |       this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
149 |           var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
153 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
154 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.NamedType.lookupViaProperties(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/NamedType.java
Original range: 285-319
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolves a type by looking up its first component in the scope, and
   * subsequent components as properties. The scope must have been fully
   * parsed and a symbol table constructed.
   * @return The type of the symbol, or null if the type could not be found.
   */
Method code:
285 |   private JSType lookupViaProperties( ErrorReporter t,
286 |       StaticScope<JSType> enclosing) {
287 |     String[] componentNames = reference.split("\\.", -1);
288 |     if (componentNames[0].length() == 0) {
289 |       return null;
290 |     }
291 |     StaticSlot<JSType> slot = enclosing.getSlot(componentNames[0]);
292 |     if (slot == null) {
293 |       return null;
294 |     }
295 |     // If the first component has a type of 'Unknown', then any type
296 |     // names using it should be regarded as silently 'Unknown' rather than be
297 |     // noisy about it.
298 |     JSType slotType = slot.getType();
299 |     if (slotType == null || slotType.isAllType() || slotType.isNoType()) {
300 |       return null;
301 |     }
302 |     JSType value = getTypedefType(t, slot, componentNames[0]);
303 |     if (value == null) {
304 |       return null;
305 |     }
306 | 
307 |     // resolving component by component
308 |     for (int i = 1; i < componentNames.length; i++) {
309 |       ObjectType parentClass = ObjectType.cast(value);
310 |       if (parentClass == null) {
311 |         return null;
312 |       }
313 |       if (componentNames[i].length() == 0) {
314 |         return null;
315 |       }
316 |       value = parentClass.getPropertyType(componentNames[i]);
317 |     }
318 |     return value;
319 |   }

514 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
515 |       switch (n.getType()) {
516 |         case Token.NULL:
517 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
518 |           break;
519 | 
520 |         case Token.VOID:
521 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
522 |           break;
523 | 
524 |         case Token.STRING:
525 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
526 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
527 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
528 |           }
529 |           break;

532 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
533 |           break;
534 | 
535 |         case Token.TRUE:
536 |         case Token.FALSE:
537 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
538 |           break;
539 | 
540 |         case Token.REGEXP:
541 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
542 |           break;
543 |

545 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
546 |           break;
547 | 
548 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
549 |           defineObjectLiteral(t, n);
550 |           break;

553 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
554 |       }

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1536-1587
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1536 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1537 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1538 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1539 | 
1540 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1541 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1542 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1543 |       return;
1544 |     }
1545 | 
1546 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1547 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1548 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1549 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1550 | 
1551 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1552 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1553 |       if(functionJSDocInfo != null) {
1554 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1555 |       }
1556 | 
1557 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1558 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1559 |       // in an extern.
1560 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1561 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1562 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1563 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1564 |            !isExtern)) {
1565 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1566 |       }
1567 | 
1568 |       // Functions with explcit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1569 |       // or GETELEM.
1570 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1571 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1572 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isNativeObjectType() &&
1573 |           !(child.getType() == Token.GETELEM ||
1574 |             child.getType() == Token.GETPROP)) {
1575 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1576 |       }
1577 | 
1578 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1579 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1580 |     } else {
1581 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1582 |     }
1583 | 
1584 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1585 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1586 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1587 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
68 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
69 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
70 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
71 |   }

===== M0090 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 503-556
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
503 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
504 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
505 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
506 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ||
507 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
508 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
509 |       if (info == null) {
510 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
511 |       }
512 |       allowDupe =
513 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
514 |     }
515 | 
516 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
517 | 
518 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
519 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
520 |     // compilation process.
521 |     if (varType != null &&
522 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
523 |         newType != null &&
524 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
525 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
526 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
527 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
528 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
529 |       // native type.
530 |       if (var.input == null) {
531 |         n.setJSType(varType);
532 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
533 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
534 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
535 |           }
536 |         } else {
537 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
538 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
539 |         }
540 |       } else {
541 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
542 |         // match the original type.
543 |         //
544 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
545 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
546 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
547 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
548 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
549 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
550 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
551 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
552 |               varType.toString()));
553 |         }
554 |       }
555 |     }
556 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.canPropertyBeDefined(JSType,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 688-698
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns whether the given property can possibly be set on the given type.
   */
Method code:
688 |   public boolean canPropertyBeDefined(JSType type, String propertyName) {
689 |     if (typesIndexedByProperty.containsKey(propertyName)) {
690 |       for (JSType alt :
691 |                typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName).getAlternates()) {
692 |         if (!alt.getGreatestSubtype(type).isEmptyType()) {
693 |           return true;
694 |         }
695 |       }
696 |     }
697 |     return false;
698 |   }

===== M0106 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 420-484
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
420 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
421 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
422 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
423 | 
424 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
425 |       // bottom scope.
426 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
427 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
428 | 
429 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
430 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
431 | 
432 |       symbols = slotsA;
433 | 
434 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
435 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
436 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
437 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
438 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
439 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
440 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
441 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
442 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
443 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
444 |       //    the two types.
445 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
446 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
447 | 
448 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
449 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
450 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
451 | 
452 |         JSType joinedType = null;
453 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
454 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
455 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
456 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
457 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
458 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
459 |           } else {
460 |             // Case #3
461 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
462 |           }
463 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
464 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
465 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
466 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
467 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
468 |             // Case #2
469 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
470 |           } else {
471 |             // Case #4
472 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
473 |           }
474 |         } else {
475 |           // Case #5
476 |           joinedType =
477 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
478 |         }
479 | 
480 |         if (joinedType != null) {
481 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
482 |         }
483 |       }
484 |     }

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 460-812
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
460 |   @Override
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.LP:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.GET_REF:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.NULL:
505 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
506 |         break;
507 | 
508 |       case Token.NUMBER:
509 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
510 |         break;
511 | 
512 |       case Token.STRING:
513 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
514 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
515 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
516 |         } else {
517 |           // Object literal keys are not typeable
518 |           typeable = false;
519 |         }
520 |         break;
521 | 
522 |       case Token.GET:
523 |       case Token.SET:
524 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
528 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
529 |         break;
530 | 
531 |       case Token.REGEXP:
532 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
533 |         break;
534 | 
535 |       case Token.GETPROP:
536 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
537 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
538 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.GETELEM:
542 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
543 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
544 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
545 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
546 |         typeable = false;
547 |         break;
548 | 
549 |       case Token.VAR:
550 |         visitVar(t, n);
551 |         typeable = false;
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.NEW:
555 |         visitNew(t, n);
556 |         typeable = true;
557 |         break;
558 | 
559 |       case Token.CALL:
560 |         visitCall(t, n);
561 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
562 |         break;
563 | 
564 |       case Token.RETURN:
565 |         visitReturn(t, n);
566 |         typeable = false;
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.DEC:
570 |       case Token.INC:
571 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
572 |         validator.expectNumber(
573 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
574 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.NOT:
578 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.VOID:
582 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
583 |         break;
584 | 
585 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
586 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.BITNOT:
590 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
591 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
592 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
593 |               childType.toString());
594 |         }
595 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
596 |         break;
597 | 
598 |       case Token.POS:
599 |       case Token.NEG:
600 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
601 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
602 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
603 |         break;
604 | 
605 |       case Token.EQ:
606 |       case Token.NE: {
607 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
608 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
609 | 
610 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
611 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
612 |         TernaryValue result =
613 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
614 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
615 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
616 |             result = result.not();
617 |           }
618 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
619 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
620 |         }
621 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
622 |         break;
623 |       }
624 | 
625 |       case Token.SHEQ:
626 |       case Token.SHNE: {
627 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
628 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
629 | 
630 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
631 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
632 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
633 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
634 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
635 |               rightType.toString());
636 |         }
637 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
638 |         break;
639 |       }
640 | 
641 |       case Token.LT:
642 |       case Token.LE:
643 |       case Token.GT:
644 |       case Token.GE:
645 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
646 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
647 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
648 |           validator.expectNumber(
649 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
650 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
651 |           validator.expectNumber(
652 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
653 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
654 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
655 |           // OK.
656 |         } else {
657 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
658 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
659 |           // should match a string context.
660 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
661 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
662 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
663 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
664 |           message = "right side of comparison";
665 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
666 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
667 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
668 |         }
669 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
670 |         break;
671 | 
672 |       case Token.IN:
673 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
674 |         right = n.getLastChild();
675 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
676 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
677 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
678 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
679 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
680 |         break;
681 | 
682 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
683 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
684 |         right = n.getLastChild();
685 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
686 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
687 | 
688 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
689 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
690 |         validator.expectActualObject(
691 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
692 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
693 |         break;
694 | 
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
696 |         visitAssign(t, n);
697 |         typeable = false;
698 |         break;
699 | 
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
703 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
704 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
705 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
706 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
707 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
708 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
709 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
710 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
711 |       case Token.LSH:
712 |       case Token.RSH:
713 |       case Token.URSH:
714 |       case Token.DIV:
715 |       case Token.MOD:
716 |       case Token.BITOR:
717 |       case Token.BITXOR:
718 |       case Token.BITAND:
719 |       case Token.SUB:
720 |       case Token.ADD:
721 |       case Token.MUL:
722 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       case Token.DELPROP:
726 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
727 |         break;
728 | 
729 |       case Token.CASE:
730 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
731 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
732 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
733 |         typeable = false;
734 |         break;
735 | 
736 |       case Token.WITH: {
737 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
738 |         childType = getJSType(child);
739 |         validator.expectObject(
740 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
741 |         typeable = false;
742 |         break;
743 |       }
744 | 
745 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
746 |         visitFunction(t, n);
747 |         break;
748 | 
749 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
750 |       case Token.LABEL:
751 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
752 |       case Token.SWITCH:
753 |       case Token.BREAK:
754 |       case Token.CATCH:
755 |       case Token.TRY:
756 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
757 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
758 |       case Token.BLOCK:
759 |       case Token.EMPTY:
760 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
761 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
762 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
763 |       case Token.THROW:
764 |         typeable = false;
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
768 |       case Token.DO:
769 |       case Token.FOR:
770 |       case Token.IF:
771 |       case Token.WHILE:
772 |         typeable = false;
773 |         break;
774 | 
775 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
776 |       case Token.AND:
777 |       case Token.HOOK:
778 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
779 |       case Token.OR:
780 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
781 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
782 |         } else {
783 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
784 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
785 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
786 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
787 |           } else {
788 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
789 |           }
790 |         }
791 |         if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
792 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
793 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
794 |           }
795 |         }
796 |         break;
797 | 
798 |       default:
799 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
800 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
801 |         break;
802 |     }
803 | 
804 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
805 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
806 | 
807 |     if (typeable) {
808 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
809 |     }
810 | 
811 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
812 |   }

/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3114 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3115 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3116 |     if (info == null) {
3117 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3118 |       if (parent == null) {
3119 |         return null;
3120 |       }
3121 | 
3122 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
3123 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
3124 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3125 |         if (info == null && parent.getParent().hasOneChild()) {

3127 |         }

3130 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {

3132 |       }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1258-1284
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1258 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1259 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1260 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1261 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1262 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1263 |       return;
1264 |     }
1265 | 
1266 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1267 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1268 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1269 |     // and what isn't.
1270 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1271 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1272 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1273 | 
1274 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1275 |     // non-object.
1276 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1277 |             childType + " has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1278 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1279 |       return;
1280 |     }
1281 | 
1282 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1283 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1284 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 231-244
Documentation:
/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
231 |   public int getMinArguments() {
232 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
233 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
234 |     // of the last required parameter.
235 |     int i = 0;
236 |     int min = 0;
237 |     for (Node n : getParameters()) {
238 |       i++;
239 |       if (!n.isOptionalArg() && !n.isVarArgs()) {
240 |         min = i;
241 |       }
242 |     }
243 |     return min;
244 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 816-858
Documentation:

Method code:
816 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
817 |       Node callNode) {
818 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
819 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
820 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
821 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
822 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
823 |       return scope;
824 |     }
825 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
826 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
827 |       return scope;
828 |     }
829 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
830 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
831 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
832 |     if (assertedType == null) {
833 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
834 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
835 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
836 |         if (type != narrowed) {
837 |           scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
838 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
839 |         }
840 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
841 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
842 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
843 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
844 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
845 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
846 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
847 |       }
848 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
849 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
850 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
851 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
852 |       if (type != narrowed) {
853 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
854 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |     return scope;
858 |   }

392 |     @Override
393 |     protected void initialize() {
394 |       orderedWorkSet.clear();
395 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node : getCfg().getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
396 |         int outEdgeCount = getCfg().getOutEdges(node.getValue()).size();

     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.

110 |   @Override
111 |   public void inferQualifiedSlot(String symbol, JSType bottomType,
112 |       JSType inferredType) {
113 |     Scope functionScope = getFunctionScope();
114 |     if (functionScope.isLocal()) {
115 |       if (functionScope.getVar(symbol) == null && !functionScope.isBottom()) {
116 |         // When we enter a local scope, many qualified names are

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1026-1127
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1026 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1027 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1028 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1029 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1030 |     // already.
1031 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1032 |       return;
1033 |     }
1034 | 
1035 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1036 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1037 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1038 |     // For interface
1039 |     boolean superInterfacesHasProperty = false;
1040 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1041 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1042 |         superInterfacesHasProperty =
1043 |           superInterfacesHasProperty || interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1044 |       }
1045 |     }
1046 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1047 | 
1048 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1049 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1050 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1051 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1052 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1053 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1054 |           continue;
1055 |         }
1056 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1057 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1058 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1059 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1060 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1061 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1062 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1063 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1064 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1065 |           // property
1066 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1067 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1068 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1069 |         }
1070 |       }
1071 |     }
1072 | 
1073 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty
1074 |         && !superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1075 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1076 |       return;
1077 |     }
1078 | 
1079 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1080 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1081 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1082 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1083 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1084 |       // property
1085 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1086 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1087 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1088 |     }
1089 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1090 |       // there's no @override to check
1091 |       return;
1092 |     }
1093 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1094 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1095 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1096 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1097 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1098 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1099 |         compiler.report(
1100 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1101 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1102 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1103 |       }
1104 |     } else if (superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1105 |       // there is an super interface property
1106 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1107 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1108 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1109 |             interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1110 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1111 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1112 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1113 |             compiler.report(
1114 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1115 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1116 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1117 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1118 |           }
1119 |         }
1120 |       }
1121 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1122 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1123 |       compiler.report(
1124 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1125 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1126 |     }
1127 |   }

===== M0193 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1635-1670
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1635 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1636 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1637 | 
1638 |     // This is a misplaced return, but the real JS will fail to compile,
1639 |     // so let it go.
1640 |     if (function == null) {
1641 |       return;
1642 |     }
1643 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(function);
1644 | 
1645 |     if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
1646 |       FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1647 | 
1648 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1649 | 
1650 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1651 |       // (it's a void function)
1652 |       if (returnType == null) {
1653 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1654 |       }
1655 | 
1656 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1657 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1658 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1659 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1660 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1661 |         valueNode = n;
1662 |       } else {
1663 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1664 |       }
1665 | 
1666 |       // verifying
1667 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1668 |           "inconsistent return type");
1669 |     }
1670 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 349-396
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
349 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
350 |     if (info != null) {
351 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
352 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
353 | 
354 |       // base type
355 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
356 |         if (isConstructor) {
357 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
358 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
359 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
360 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
361 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
362 |           }
363 |         } else {
364 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       // implemented interfaces
369 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
370 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
371 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
372 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
373 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
374 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
375 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
376 |           }
377 |         }
378 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
379 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
380 |       }
381 | 
382 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
383 |       if (isInterface) {
384 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
385 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
386 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
387 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
388 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
389 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
390 |           }
391 |         }
392 |       }
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     return this;
396 |   }

===== M0205 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 452-481
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
452 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
453 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
454 |     int type = n.getType();
455 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
456 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
457 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
458 |     }
459 | 
460 |     curNode = n;
461 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
462 | 
463 |     switch (type) {
464 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
465 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
466 |         break;
467 | 
468 |       default:
469 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
470 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
471 |           // would no longer point to the true next
472 |           Node next = child.getNext();
473 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
474 |           child = next;
475 |         }
476 |         break;
477 |     }
478 | 
479 |     curNode = n;
480 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
481 |   }

===== M0207 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 129-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
129 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
130 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
131 |     String methodName = null;
132 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
133 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
134 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
135 |       String name = callName.getString();
136 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
137 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
138 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
139 |       }
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     if (methodName != null) {
143 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
144 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
145 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
146 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
147 |       }
148 |     }
149 |     return null;
150 |   }

===== M0215 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1988-1990
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1988 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1989 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1990 |   }

/**
   * @param key A OBJECTLIT key node.
   * @return The type expected when using the key.
   */
Method code:
2160 |   static JSType getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node key, JSType valueType) {
2161 |     if (valueType != null) {
2162 |       switch (key.getType()) {
2163 |         case Token.GET:
2164 |           // GET must always return a function type.
2165 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2166 |             FunctionType fntype = valueType.toMaybeFunctionType();
2167 |             valueType = fntype.getReturnType();
2168 |           } else {
2169 |             return null;
2170 |           }
2171 |           break;
2172 |         case Token.SET:
2173 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2174 |             // SET must always return a function type.
2175 |             FunctionType fntype = valueType.toMaybeFunctionType();
2176 |             Node param = fntype.getParametersNode().getFirstChild();
2177 |             // SET function must always have one parameter.
2178 |             valueType = param.getJSType();
2179 |           } else {
2180 |             return null;
2181 |           }
2182 |           break;
2183 |       }
2184 |     }
2185 |     return valueType;
2186 |   }

===== M0226 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 616-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
616 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
617 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
618 |     if (typeSet == null) {
619 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
620 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
624 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
625 | 
626 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
627 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
628 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

Method code:
354 |   @Override
355 |   public String toString() {
356 |     if (hasReferenceName()) {
357 |       return getReferenceName();
358 |     } else if (prettyPrint) {
359 |       // Don't pretty print recursively.
360 |       prettyPrint = false;
361 | 
362 |       // Use a tree set so that the properties are sorted.
363 |       Set<String> propertyNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
364 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
365 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
366 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;
367 |            current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
368 |         propertyNames.addAll(current.getOwnPropertyNames());
369 |       }
370 | 
371 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
372 |       sb.append("{");
373 | 
374 |       int i = 0;
375 |       for (String property : propertyNames) {
376 |         if (i > 0) {
377 |           sb.append(", ");
378 |         }
379 | 
380 |         sb.append(property);
381 |         sb.append(": ");
382 |         sb.append(getPropertyType(property).toString());
383 | 
384 |         ++i;
385 |         if (i == MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES) {
386 |           sb.append(", ...");
387 |           break;
388 |         }
389 |       }
390 | 
391 |       sb.append("}");
392 | 
393 |       prettyPrint = true;
394 |       return sb.toString();
395 |     } else {
396 |       return "{...}";
397 |     }
398 |   }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 953-980
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
953 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
954 |     // unknown
955 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
956 |       return true;
957 |     }
958 |     // equality
959 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
960 |       return true;
961 |     }
962 |     // all type
963 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
964 |       return true;
965 |     }
966 |     // unions
967 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
968 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
969 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
970 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
971 |           return true;
972 |         }
973 |       }
974 |     }
975 |     // named types
976 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
977 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
978 |     }
979 |     return false;
980 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 875-900
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
875 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
876 |     int i = 0;
877 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
878 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
879 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
880 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
881 |         return;
882 |       }
883 | 
884 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
885 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
886 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
887 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
888 | 
889 |       if (iParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
890 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = iParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
891 | 
892 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
893 |             iArgumentType.isFunctionType() &&
894 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
895 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
896 |         }
897 |       }
898 |       i++;
899 |     }
900 |   }

===== M0250 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1531-1566
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1531 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1532 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1533 | 
1534 |     skipEOLs();
1535 | 
1536 |     boolean restArg = false;
1537 |     token = next();
1538 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1539 |       token = next();
1540 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1541 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1542 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1543 |       }
1544 |       restArg = true;
1545 |     }
1546 | 
1547 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1548 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1549 |       skipEOLs();
1550 |       if (restArg) {
1551 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1552 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1553 |         next();
1554 |         skipEOLs();
1555 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1556 |       }
1557 | 
1558 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1559 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1560 |       } else {
1561 |         next();
1562 |       }
1563 |     }
1564 | 
1565 |     return typeNode;
1566 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 486-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
486 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
487 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
488 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
489 | 
490 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
491 | 
492 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
493 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
494 | 
495 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
496 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
497 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
498 |     }
499 | 
500 |     curNode = n;
501 |     pushScope(n);
502 | 
503 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
504 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
505 |       // scope.
506 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
510 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
511 | 
512 |     // Args
513 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
514 | 
515 |     // Body
516 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
517 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
518 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
519 | 
520 |     popScope();
521 |   }

270 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
271 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
272 |       return;
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     try {
276 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
277 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
278 | 
279 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
280 |       sourceName = "";
281 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
282 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
283 | 
284 |       for (Node root : roots) {
285 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
286 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
287 |       }
288 | 
289 |       popScope();
290 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
291 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
292 |     }
293 |   }
