===== M0288 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-196
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |       case Token.FINALLY:
158 |         return true;
159 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
160 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
161 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
162 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
163 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
164 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
165 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
166 |         // child.
167 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
168 |       case Token.WHILE:
169 |       case Token.DO:
170 |       case Token.IF:
171 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
172 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
173 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.FOR:
176 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
177 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
178 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
179 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
180 |         // That way the following:
181 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
182 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
183 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
184 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
185 |         } else {
186 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
187 |         }
188 |       case Token.SWITCH:
189 |       case Token.CASE:
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |       case Token.WITH:
192 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
193 |       default:
194 |         return false;
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 260-310
Documentation:

Method code:
260 |   @Override
261 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
262 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
263 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
264 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
265 |         return true;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
269 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
270 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
271 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
272 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
273 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
274 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
275 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
276 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
277 |         return false;
278 |       }
279 | 
280 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
281 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
282 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
283 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
284 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
285 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
286 |             return false;
287 |           }
288 |         }
289 | 
290 |         return true;
291 |       }
292 | 
293 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
294 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
295 | 
296 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
297 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
298 |           return false;
299 |         }
300 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
301 |       }
302 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
303 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
304 |           return false;
305 |         }
306 |       }
307 |       return true;
308 |     }
309 |     return false;
310 |   }

   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.

Method code:
2748 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2749 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2750 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2751 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2752 |       return true;
2753 |     }

2763 |     }

===== M0314 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 719-745
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infimums, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
719 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
720 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
721 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
722 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
723 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
724 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
725 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
726 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
727 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
728 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
729 |           needsFiltering = true;
730 |           break;
731 |         }
732 |       }
733 | 
734 |       if (needsFiltering) {
735 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
736 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
737 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
738 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
739 |           }
740 |         }
741 |         return builder.build();
742 |       }
743 |     }
744 |     return type;
745 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateMinimumChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 790-805

Method code:
790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {
793 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
794 |     } else if (i == 2) {
795 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
796 |     } else {
797 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     if (!valid) {
801 |       violation(
802 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }
805 |   }

===== M0321 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2513-2523
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2513 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2514 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2515 |     int len = s.length();
2516 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2517 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2518 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2519 |         return false;
2520 |       }
2521 |     }
2522 |     return true;
2523 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
877 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
878 |     if (item == null) {
879 |       return null;
880 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
881 |       return item.getNext();
882 |     } else {

887 |         return item;
888 |       }
889 |     }

===== M0358 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
900 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
901 |     switch (n.getType()) {
902 |       case Token.CALL:
903 |       case Token.GETPROP:
904 |       case Token.GETELEM:
905 |       case Token.THROW:
906 |       case Token.NEW:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
908 |       case Token.INC:
909 |       case Token.DEC:
910 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
911 |         return true;
912 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
913 |         return false;
914 |     }
915 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
916 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
917 |         return true;
918 |       }
919 |     }
920 |     return false;
921 |   }

===== M0369 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.getReadableJSTypeName(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 680-730
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node, get a human-readable name for the type of that node so
   * that will be easy for the programmer to find the original declaration.
   *
   * For example, if SubFoo's property "bar" might have the human-readable
   * name "Foo.prototype.bar".
   *
   * @param n The node.
   * @param dereference If true, the type of the node will be dereferenced
   *     to an Object type, if possible.
   */
Method code:
680 |   String getReadableJSTypeName(Node n, boolean dereference) {
681 |     // If we're analyzing a GETPROP, the property may be inherited by the
682 |     // prototype chain. So climb the prototype chain and find out where
683 |     // the property was originally defined.
684 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
685 |       ObjectType objectType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild()).dereference();
686 |       if (objectType != null) {
687 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
688 |         if (objectType.getConstructor() != null &&
689 |             objectType.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
690 |           objectType = FunctionType.getTopDefiningInterface(
691 |               objectType, propName);
692 |         } else {
693 |           // classes
694 |           while (objectType != null && !objectType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
695 |             objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
696 |           }
697 |         }
698 | 
699 |         // Don't show complex function names or anonymous types.
700 |         // Instead, try to get a human-readable type name.
701 |         if (objectType != null &&
702 |             (objectType.getConstructor() != null ||
703 |              objectType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
704 |           return objectType.toString() + "." + propName;
705 |         }
706 |       }
707 |     }
708 | 
709 |     JSType type = getJSType(n);
710 |     if (dereference) {
711 |       ObjectType dereferenced = type.dereference();
712 |       if (dereferenced != null) {
713 |         type = dereferenced;
714 |       }
715 |     }
716 | 
717 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
718 |     if (type.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
719 |         (type.toObjectType() != null &&
720 |          type.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null)) {
721 |       return type.toString();
722 |     } else if (qualifiedName != null) {
723 |       return qualifiedName;
724 |     } else if (type.isFunctionType()) {
725 |       // Don't show complex function names.
726 |       return "function";
727 |     } else {
728 |       return type.toString();
729 |     }
730 |   }

/**

   * If true, we will not emit warnings about a missing property.

===== M0382 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1373-1489
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1373 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1374 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1375 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1376 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1377 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1378 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1379 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1380 | 
1381 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1382 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1383 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1384 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1385 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1386 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1387 |       //
1388 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1389 |       // the function has jsdoc or has not been declared before.
1390 |       //
1391 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1392 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1393 | 
1394 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1395 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1396 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1397 |         // Determining type for #5
1398 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1399 |       }
1400 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1401 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1402 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1403 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1404 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1405 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1406 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1407 |         if (qVar != null) {
1408 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1409 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1410 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1411 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1412 |           // the @extends tag.
1413 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1414 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1415 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1416 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1417 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1418 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1419 |             return;
1420 |           }
1421 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1422 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1423 |           }
1424 |         }
1425 |       }
1426 | 
1427 |       if (valueType == null) {
1428 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1429 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1430 |               n,
1431 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1432 |               ownerName));
1433 |         }
1434 | 
1435 |         return;
1436 |       }
1437 | 
1438 |       boolean inferred = true;
1439 |       if (info != null) {
1440 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3 + #4
1441 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1442 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1443 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1444 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1445 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1446 |       }
1447 | 
1448 |       if (inferred) {
1449 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1450 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1451 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1452 |             (info != null || !scope.isDeclared(qName, false)));
1453 |       }
1454 | 
1455 |       if (!inferred) {
1456 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1457 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1458 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1459 |           // declared yet.
1460 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1461 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1462 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1463 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1464 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1465 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1466 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1467 |           }
1468 |         }
1469 | 
1470 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1471 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1472 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1473 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1474 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1475 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1476 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1477 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1478 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1479 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1480 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1481 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1482 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1483 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1484 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1485 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1486 |           }
1487 |         }
1488 |       }
1489 |     }

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.detectImplicitPrototypeCycle()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 161-181
Documentation:
/**
   * Detects a cycle in the implicit prototype chain. This method accesses
   * the {@link #getImplicitPrototype()} method and must therefore be
   * invoked only after the object is sufficiently initialized to respond to
   * calls to this method.<p>
   *
   * The method is not thread safe.<p>
   *
   * @return True iff an implicit prototype cycle was detected.
   */
Method code:
161 |   final boolean detectImplicitPrototypeCycle() {
162 |     // detecting cycle
163 |     this.visited = true;
164 |     ObjectType p = getImplicitPrototype();
165 |     while (p != null) {
166 |       if (p.visited) {
167 |         return true;
168 |       } else {
169 |         p.visited = true;
170 |       }
171 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
172 |     }
173 | 
174 |     // clean up
175 |     p = this;
176 |     do {
177 |       p.visited = false;
178 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
179 |     } while (p != null);
180 |     return false;
181 |   }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 239-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
239 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
240 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
241 |     if (oldType == null) {
242 |       return this;
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
246 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
247 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
248 |       // Not a function literal.
249 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
250 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
251 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
252 |       }
253 |     } else {
254 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
255 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
256 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
257 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
258 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
259 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
260 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
261 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
262 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
263 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
264 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
265 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
266 | 
267 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
268 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
269 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
270 | 
271 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
272 |           // arguments.
273 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
274 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
275 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
276 |           }
277 |         } else {
278 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
279 |               paramBuilder,
280 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
281 |               warnedAboutArgList,
282 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
283 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
284 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
285 |         }
286 |       }
287 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
288 |     }
289 |     return this;
290 |   }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-217
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |         return n.getString();
182 | 
183 |       case Token.NAME:
184 |         String name = n.getString();
185 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
186 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
187 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
188 |           return name;
189 |         }
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NUMBER:
193 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
194 | 
195 |       case Token.FALSE:
196 |       case Token.TRUE:
197 |       case Token.NULL:
198 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
199 | 
200 |       case Token.VOID:
201 |         return "undefined";
202 | 
203 |       case Token.NOT:
204 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
205 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
206 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
207 |         }
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
211 |         return arrayToString(n);
212 | 
213 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
214 |         return "[object Object]";
215 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
348 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
349 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
350 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
351 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
352 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
353 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
354 |         // the line numbers of both types.
355 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType, null);
356 |       } else {
357 |         mismatch(t, n,
358 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
359 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
360 |             rightType, leftType);
361 |       }
362 |       return false;
363 |     }
364 |     return true;
365 |   }

===== M0435 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 99-160
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
99 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
100 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
101 |     // just bail out early here.
102 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
103 |       return this;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
107 | 
108 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
109 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
110 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
111 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
112 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
113 |     }
114 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
115 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
116 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
117 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
118 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
119 |         }
120 |       } else {
121 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
122 |           return this;
123 |         }
124 | 
125 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
126 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
127 |         // one another.
128 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
129 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
130 |           JSType current = it.next();
131 | 
132 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
133 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
134 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
135 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
136 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
137 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
138 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
139 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
140 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
141 |               return this;
142 |             }
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
145 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
146 |               return this;
147 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
148 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
149 |               it.remove();
150 |             }
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |         alternates.add(alternate);
154 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       result = null;
158 |     }
159 |     return this;
160 |   }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1133-1155
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1133 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1134 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1135 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1136 | 
1137 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1138 |     // and break the cycle.
1139 |     while (true) {
1140 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1141 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1142 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1143 |         return false;
1144 |       }
1145 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1146 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1147 |         return true;
1148 |       }
1149 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1150 |       if (ctor == null) {
1151 |         return false;
1152 |       }
1153 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1154 |     }
1155 |   }

357 |   private void handleIf(Node node) {
358 |     Node thenBlock = node.getFirstChild().getNext();
359 |     Node elseBlock = thenBlock.getNext();
360 |     createEdge(node, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(thenBlock));
361 |

364 |           computeFollowNode(node, this)); // not taken branch

369 |         node, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 322-350
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype, creating the prototype object from the given
   * base type.
   * @param baseType The base type.
   */
Method code:
322 |   public void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType) {
323 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
324 |     // two cases:
325 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
326 |     // and
327 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
328 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
329 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
330 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
331 |     //
332 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
333 |     //
334 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
335 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
336 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
337 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
338 |     //
339 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
340 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
341 |         baseType.isUnknownType() ||
342 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
343 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
344 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
345 | 
346 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
347 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
348 |     }
349 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType);
350 |   }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.normalizeObjectLiteralKeyAnnotations(Node,Node,Node)

Original range: 222-229
Documentation:
/**
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1592-1626
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1592 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1593 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1594 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1595 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1596 | 
1597 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1598 |     int ordinal = 0;
1599 |     Node parameter = null;
1600 |     Node argument = null;
1601 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1602 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1603 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1604 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1605 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1606 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1607 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1608 |       }
1609 |       argument = arguments.next();
1610 |       ordinal++;
1611 | 
1612 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1613 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1614 |     }
1615 | 
1616 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1617 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1618 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1619 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1620 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1621 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1622 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1623 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1624 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1625 |     }
1626 |   }

===== M0484 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 572-602
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
572 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
573 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
574 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
575 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
576 |     if (isOptional) {
577 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
578 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
579 |       // error can be reported.
580 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
581 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
582 |         emittedWarning = true;
583 |       }
584 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
585 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
586 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
587 |         emittedWarning = true;
588 |       }
589 |     } else {
590 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
591 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
592 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
593 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
594 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
595 |         } else {
596 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
597 |         }
598 |         emittedWarning = true;
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 |     return emittedWarning;
602 |   }

===== M0493 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 544-589
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
544 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
545 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
546 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
547 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
548 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
549 |     if (objectType == null) {
550 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
551 |     } else {
552 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
553 |         return;
554 |       }
555 | 
556 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
557 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
558 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
559 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
560 |         //
561 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
562 |         //    inferred type.
563 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
564 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
565 |         //    define it.
566 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
567 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
568 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
569 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
570 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
571 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
572 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
573 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
574 |           } else {
575 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
576 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
577 |           }
578 |         } else {
579 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
580 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
581 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
582 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
583 |           } else {
584 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
585 |           }
586 |         }
587 |       }
588 |     }
589 |   }

===== M0496 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 631-670
Documentation:

Method code:
631 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
632 |     String varName = n.getString();
633 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
634 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
635 |     if (value != null) {
636 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
637 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
638 |           getJSType(value));
639 |       return scope;
640 |     } else {
641 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
642 |       if (var != null) {
643 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
644 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
645 | 
646 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
647 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
648 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
649 |         boolean unflowable =
650 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
651 | 
652 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
653 |         // inferred variable.
654 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
655 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
656 |             isInferred &&
657 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
658 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
659 | 
660 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
661 |           type = var.getType();
662 |           if (type == null) {
663 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
664 |           }
665 |         }
666 |       }
667 |     }
668 |     n.setJSType(type);
669 |     return scope;
670 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 679-721

Method code:
679 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
680 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
681 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(type);
682 | 
683 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null; name = name.getNext()) {
684 |       scope = traverse(name.getFirstChild(), scope);
685 |     }
686 | 
687 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types, or changed with
688 |     // type casts.
689 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
690 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
691 |     // TODO(nicksantos): There should be an "anonymous object" type that
692 |     // we can check for here.
693 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
694 |     if (objectType == null) {
695 |       return scope;
696 |     }
697 | 
698 |     boolean hasLendsName = n.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
699 |         n.getJSDocInfo().getLendsName() != null;
700 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName() && !hasLendsName) {
701 |       return scope;
702 |     }
703 | 
704 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
705 |          name = name.getNext()) {
706 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
707 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(name);
708 |       if (memberName != null) {
709 |         JSType rawValueType =  name.getFirstChild().getJSType();
710 |         JSType valueType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
711 |             name, rawValueType);
712 |         if (valueType == null) {
713 |           valueType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
714 |         }
715 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, valueType, name);
716 |       } else {
717 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
718 |       }
719 |     }
720 |     return scope;
721 |   }

   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no

430 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
431 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
432 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
433 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 601-659
Documentation:

Method code:
601 |     @Override
602 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
603 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
604 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
605 |       if (name == null) {
606 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
607 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
608 |           errorReporter.error(
609 |             "unnamed function statement",
610 |             sourceName,
611 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
612 |         }
613 |         name = new Name();
614 |         name.setIdentifier("");
615 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
616 |       }
617 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
618 |       Node newName = transform(name);
619 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
620 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
621 |         // declaration.
622 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
623 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
624 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
625 |         // same line as the function keyword.
626 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
627 |             functionNode.getLp();
628 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
629 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
630 |       }
631 | 
632 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
633 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
634 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
635 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
636 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
637 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
638 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
639 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
640 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
641 |       if (fnName != null) {
642 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
643 |       } else {
644 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
645 |       }
646 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
647 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
648 | 
649 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
650 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
651 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
652 |       }
653 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
654 | 
655 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
656 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
657 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
658 |      return node;
659 |     }

===== M0512 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1466-1527
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1466 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1467 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1468 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1469 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1470 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1471 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1472 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1473 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1474 |         compiler.report(
1475 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1476 |       } else {
1477 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1478 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1479 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1480 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1481 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1482 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1483 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1484 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1485 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1486 |               badImplementedType = true;
1487 |             }
1488 |           } else {
1489 |             badImplementedType = true;
1490 |           }
1491 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1492 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1493 |           }
1494 |         }
1495 |         // check properties
1496 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1497 |       }
1498 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1499 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1500 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1501 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1502 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1503 |           compiler.report(
1504 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1505 |         }
1506 |       }
1507 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1508 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1509 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1510 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1511 |       }
1512 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1513 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1514 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1515 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1516 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1517 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1518 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1519 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1520 |           currentProperties.clear();
1521 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1522 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1523 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1524 |         }
1525 |       }
1526 |     }
1527 |   }

Original range: 495-547

Method code:
495 |   @Override
496 |   public JSType getPropertyType(String name) {
497 |     if (!hasOwnProperty(name)) {
498 |       if ("call".equals(name)) {
499 |         // Define the "call" function lazily.
500 |         Node params = getParametersNode();
501 |         if (params == null) {
502 |           // If there's no params array, don't do any type-checking
503 |           // in this CALL function.
504 |           defineDeclaredProperty(name,
505 |               new FunctionBuilder(registry)
506 |               .withReturnType(getReturnType())
507 |               .build(),
508 |               source);
509 |         } else {
510 |           params = params.cloneTree();
511 |           Node thisTypeNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "thisType");
512 |           thisTypeNode.setJSType(
513 |               registry.createOptionalNullableType(getTypeOfThis()));
514 |           params.addChildToFront(thisTypeNode);
515 |           thisTypeNode.setOptionalArg(true);
516 | 
517 |           defineDeclaredProperty(name,
518 |               new FunctionBuilder(registry)
519 |               .withParamsNode(params)
520 |               .withReturnType(getReturnType())
521 |               .build(),
522 |               source);
523 |         }
524 |       } else if ("apply".equals(name)) {
525 |         // Define the "apply" function lazily.
526 |         FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(registry);
527 | 
528 |         // Ecma-262 says that apply's second argument must be an Array
529 |         // or an arguments object. We don't model the arguments object,
530 |         // so let's just be forgiving for now.
531 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Model the Arguments object.
532 |         builder.addOptionalParams(
533 |             registry.createNullableType(getTypeOfThis()),
534 |             registry.createNullableType(
535 |                 registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE)));
536 | 
537 |         defineDeclaredProperty(name,
538 |             new FunctionBuilder(registry)
539 |             .withParams(builder)
540 |             .withReturnType(getReturnType())
541 |             .build(),
542 |             source);
543 |       }
544 |     }
545 | 
546 |     return super.getPropertyType(name);
547 |   }

/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>

   */

634 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
635 |       return that.getLeastSupertype(this);
636 |     }
637 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
638 |   }

/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string

1938 |   public String getQualifiedName() {

1947 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1948 |       return "this";
1949 |     } else {
1950 |       return null;
1951 |     }
1952 |   }

/**
   * Resolves a named type by looking up its first component in the scope, and
   * subsequent components as properties. The scope must have been fully
   * parsed and a symbol table constructed.
   */
Method code:
257 |   private void resolveViaProperties(ErrorReporter t,
258 |                                     StaticScope<JSType> enclosing) {
259 |     JSType value = lookupViaProperties(t, enclosing);
260 |     // last component of the chain
261 |     if (value != null && (value.isConstructor() || value.isInterface())) {
262 |       FunctionType functionType = value.toMaybeFunctionType();
263 |       setReferencedAndResolvedType(
264 |           functionType.getInstanceType(), t, enclosing);
265 |     } else if (value instanceof EnumType) {
266 |       setReferencedAndResolvedType(
267 |           ((EnumType) value).getElementsType(), t, enclosing);
268 |     } else {
269 |       // We've been running into issues where people forward-declare
270 |       // non-named types. (This is legitimate...our dependency management
271 |       // code doubles as our forward-declaration code.)
272 |       //
273 |       // So if the type does resolve to an actual value, but it's not named,
274 |       // then don't respect the forward declaration.
275 |       handleUnresolvedType(t, value == null || value.isUnknownType());
276 |     }
277 |   }

/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
486 |   @Override
487 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
488 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
489 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
490 |     if (unknown) {
491 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
492 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
493 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
494 |         unknown = false;
495 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
496 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
497 |             unknown = true;
498 |             break;
499 |           }
500 |         }
501 |       } else {
502 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return unknown;
506 |   }

===== M0535 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1152-1290
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1152 |   Node parseInputs() {
1153 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1154 | 
1155 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1156 |     // individual file parse trees.
1157 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1158 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1159 |     }
1160 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1161 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1162 |     }
1163 | 
1164 |     // Parse main js sources.
1165 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1166 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1167 | 
1168 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1169 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1170 | 
1171 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1172 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1173 | 
1174 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1175 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1176 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1177 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1178 |     }
1179 | 
1180 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1181 | 
1182 |     try {
1183 |       // Parse externs sources.
1184 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1185 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1186 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1187 |           return null;
1188 |         }
1189 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1190 |       }
1191 | 
1192 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1193 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1194 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1195 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1196 | 
1197 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1198 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1199 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1200 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1201 |           }
1202 |         }
1203 | 
1204 |         try {
1205 |           inputs =
1206 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1207 |               .manageDependencies(
1208 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1209 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1210 |           report(JSError.make(
1211 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1212 |           return null;
1213 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1214 |           report(JSError.make(
1215 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1216 |           return null;
1217 |         }
1218 |       }
1219 | 
1220 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1221 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1222 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1223 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1224 | 
1225 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1226 |         if (n == null) {
1227 |           continue;
1228 |         }
1229 | 
1230 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1231 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1232 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1233 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1234 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1235 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1236 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1237 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1238 | 
1239 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1240 | 
1241 |             externs.add(input);
1242 |             staleInputs = true;
1243 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1244 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1245 |             staleInputs = true;
1246 |           }
1247 |         }
1248 |       }
1249 | 
1250 |       if (staleInputs) {
1251 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1252 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1253 |       }
1254 | 
1255 |       // Build the AST.
1256 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1257 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1258 |         if (n == null) {
1259 |           continue;
1260 |         }
1261 | 
1262 |         if (devMode) {
1263 |           runSanityCheck();
1264 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1265 |             return null;
1266 |           }
1267 |         }
1268 | 
1269 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1270 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1271 | 
1272 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1273 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1274 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1275 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1276 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1277 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1278 |         }
1279 | 
1280 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1281 |       }
1282 | 
1283 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1284 |         return null;
1285 |       }
1286 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1287 |     } finally {
1288 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1289 |     }
1290 |   }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

     */

456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)

478 |          * single 's.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)

/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1044 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1045 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1046 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1047 | 
1048 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.getType() == Token.NAME && scope.isGlobal();
1049 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1050 |           isGlobalVar &&
1051 |           (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
1052 |            parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
1053 | 
1054 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1055 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1056 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1057 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1058 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1059 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1060 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1061 | 
1062 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1063 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1064 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1065 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1066 |         }
1067 |       }
1068 | 
1069 |       // declared in closest scope?
1070 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1071 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1072 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1073 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1074 |       } else {
1075 |         if (!inferred) {
1076 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1077 |         }
1078 | 
1079 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1080 |         // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet.
1081 |         boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1082 |         Var newVar =
1083 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1084 | 
1085 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1086 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1087 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1088 |               (initialValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT ||
1089 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1090 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1091 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1092 |           }
1093 |         }
1094 | 
1095 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1096 |         FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1097 |         if (fnType != null &&
1098 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1099 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1100 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1101 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1102 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1103 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1104 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1105 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), input,
1106 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1107 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1108 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1109 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1110 | 
1111 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1112 |             // it constructs itself.
1113 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1114 |                 !isExtern &&
1115 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1116 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1117 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1118 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1119 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1120 |                 // this.
1121 |                 variableName.equals(
1122 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1123 |               compiler.report(
1124 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1125 |                       fnType.isConstructor() ?
1126 |                           CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1127 |                       variableName));
1128 |             }
1129 |           }
1130 |         }
1131 |       }
1132 | 
1133 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1134 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1135 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1136 |         if (inferred) {
1137 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1138 |               type == null ?
1139 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1140 |               type,
1141 |               n);
1142 |         } else {
1143 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1144 |         }
1145 |       }
1146 | 
1147 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1148 |           && type != null
1149 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1150 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1151 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1152 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1153 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1154 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1155 |         globalThisCtor
1156 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1157 |       }
1158 |     }
