Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }
1233 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
2077 |   public Node cloneTree() {
2078 |     Node result = cloneNode();
2079 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
2080 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
2081 |       n2clone.parent = result;
2082 |       if (result.last != null) {
2083 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
2084 |       }
2085 |       if (result.first == null) {
2086 |         result.first = n2clone;
2087 |       }
2088 |       result.last = n2clone;
2089 |     }
2090 |     return result;
2091 |   }

===== M0586 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0593 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1781-1844
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1781 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1782 |     if (type != node.getType()
1783 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1784 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1785 |       return false;
1786 |     }
1787 | 
1788 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1789 |       return false;
1790 |     }
1791 | 
1792 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1793 |       try {
1794 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1795 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1796 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1797 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1798 |             return false;
1799 |           }
1800 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1801 |           return false;
1802 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1803 |           return false;
1804 |         } else {
1805 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1806 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1807 |               return false;
1808 |             }
1809 |           }
1810 |         }
1811 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1812 |         return false;
1813 |       }
1814 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1815 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1816 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1817 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1818 |         return false;
1819 |       }
1820 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1821 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1822 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1823 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1824 |         return false;
1825 |       }
1826 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1827 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1828 |         return false;
1829 |       }
1830 |     }
1831 | 
1832 |     if (recurse) {
1833 |       Node n, n2;
1834 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1835 |            n != null;
1836 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1837 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1838 |           return false;
1839 |         }
1840 |       }
1841 |     }
1842 | 
1843 |     return true;
1844 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
173 |   boolean parse() {
174 |     int lineno;
175 |     int charno;
176 | 
177 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
178 |     JSTypeExpression type;
179 | 
180 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
181 |     skipEOLs();
182 | 
183 |     JsDocToken token = next();
184 | 
185 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
186 | 
187 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
188 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
189 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
190 |       token = blockInfo.token;
191 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
192 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
193 |       }
194 |     } else {
195 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
196 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
197 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
198 |         // what it was.
199 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
200 |       }
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
204 |     retry: for (;;) {
205 |       switch (token) {
206 |         case ANNOTATION:
207 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
208 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
209 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
210 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
211 | 
212 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
213 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
214 |             if (annotation == null) {
215 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
216 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
217 |             } else {
218 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
219 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
220 | 
221 |               switch (annotation) {
222 |                 case AUTHOR:
223 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
224 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
225 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
226 | 
227 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
228 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
229 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
230 |                     } else {
231 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
232 |                     }
233 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
234 |                   } else {
235 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
236 |                   }
237 |                   continue retry;
238 | 
239 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
240 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
241 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
242 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
243 |                   }
244 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
245 |                   continue retry;
246 | 
247 |                 case CONSTANT:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
250 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;
254 | 
255 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
257 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
258 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
259 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                     } else {
261 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
262 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
263 |                     }
264 |                   }
265 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
266 |                   continue retry;
267 | 
268 |                 case DEPRECATED:
269 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
270 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
271 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
272 |                   }
273 | 
274 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
275 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
276 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
277 | 
278 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
279 | 
280 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
281 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
282 |                   }
283 | 
284 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
285 |                   continue retry;
286 | 
287 |                 case INTERFACE:
288 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
289 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
290 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
291 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
292 |                     } else {
293 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
294 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
295 |                     }
296 |                   }

298 |                   continue retry;

300 |                 case DESC:
301 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
302 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
303 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
304 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
305 |                     continue retry;
306 |                   } else {

308 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);

605 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {

610 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
611 |                         lineno, charno);

616 |                       token = next();

619 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
620 |                       // system.
621 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
622 |                         token = next();
623 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
624 |                           token = next();

626 |                       }

629 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
630 |                       } else if (type != null) {
631 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
632 |                         // already.
633 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
634 |                       }
635 |                     }
636 | 
637 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
638 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
639 |                     // for handling properties of params.
640 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
641 |                       name = null;
642 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
643 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
644 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
645 |                             lineno, charno);
646 |                       } else {
647 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
648 |                             lineno, charno);
649 |                       }
650 |                     }
651 |                   }
652 | 
653 |                   if (name == null) {
654 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
655 |                     continue retry;
656 |                   }
657 | 
658 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
659 | 
660 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
661 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
662 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
663 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
664 | 
665 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
666 | 
667 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
668 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
669 |                           paramDescription);
670 |                     }
671 | 
672 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
673 |                   } else {
674 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
675 |                   }
676 |                   continue retry;
677 | 
678 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
679 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
680 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
681 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
682 |                   }
683 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
684 |                   continue retry;
685 | 
686 |                 case PRIVATE:
687 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
688 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
689 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
690 |                   }
691 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
692 |                   continue retry;
693 | 
694 |                 case PROTECTED:
695 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
696 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
697 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
698 |                   }
699 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
700 |                   continue retry;
701 | 
702 |                 case PUBLIC:
703 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
704 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
705 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
706 |                   }
707 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
708 |                   continue retry;
709 | 
710 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
711 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
712 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
713 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
714 |                   }
715 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
716 |                   continue retry;
717 | 
718 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
719 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
720 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
721 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
722 |                   }
723 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
724 |                   continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                 case MODIFIES:
727 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
728 |                   continue retry;
729 | 
730 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
731 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
732 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
733 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
734 |                   }
735 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
736 |                   continue retry;
737 | 
738 |                 case SEE:
739 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
740 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
741 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
742 | 
743 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
744 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
745 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
746 |                     } else {
747 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
748 |                     }
749 | 
750 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
751 |                   } else {
752 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
753 |                   }
754 |                   continue retry;
755 | 
756 |                 case SUPPRESS:
757 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
758 |                   continue retry;
759 | 
760 |                 case TEMPLATE:
761 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
762 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
763 | 
764 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
765 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
766 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
767 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
768 |                       templateTypeName)) {
769 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
770 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
771 |                   }
772 | 
773 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
774 |                   continue retry;
775 | 
776 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
777 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
778 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
779 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
780 |                   }
781 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
782 |                   continue retry;
783 | 
784 |                 case VERSION:
785 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
786 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
787 | 
788 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
789 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
790 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
791 |                   } else {
792 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
793 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
794 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
795 |                     }
796 |                   }
797 | 
798 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
799 |                   continue retry;
800 | 
801 |                 case DEFINE:
802 |                 case RETURN:
803 |                 case THIS:
804 |                 case TYPE:
805 |                 case TYPEDEF:
806 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
807 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
808 | 
809 |                   Node typeNode = null;
810 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
811 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
812 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
813 |                     // it as the unknown type.
814 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
815 |                   } else {
816 |                     skipEOLs();
817 |                     token = next();
818 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
819 |                   }
820 | 
821 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
822 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
823 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
824 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
825 |                     }
826 |                   }
827 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
828 | 
829 |                   if (type == null) {
830 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
831 |                     // recovering parsing
832 |                   } else {
833 |                     switch (annotation) {
834 |                       case DEFINE:
835 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
836 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
837 |                               lineno, charno);
838 |                         }
839 |                         break;
840 | 
841 |                       case RETURN:
842 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
843 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
844 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
845 |                           break;
846 |                         }
847 | 
848 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
849 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
850 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
851 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
852 | 
853 |                           String returnDescription =
854 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
855 | 
856 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
857 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
858 |                                 returnDescription);
859 |                           }
860 | 
861 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
862 |                         } else {
863 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
864 |                         }
865 |                         continue retry;
866 | 
867 |                       case THIS:
868 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
869 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
870 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
871 |                         }
872 |                         break;
873 | 
874 |                       case TYPE:
875 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
876 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
877 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
878 |                         }
879 |                         break;
880 | 
881 |                       case TYPEDEF:
882 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
883 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
884 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
885 |                         }
886 |                         break;
887 |                     }
888 |                   }
889 | 
890 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
891 |                   continue retry;
892 |               }
893 |             }
894 |           }
895 |           break;
896 | 
897 |         case EOC:
898 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
899 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
900 |           }
901 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
902 |           return true;
903 | 
904 |         case EOF:
905 |           // discard any accumulated information
906 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
907 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
908 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
909 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
910 |           return false;
911 | 
912 |         case EOL:
913 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
914 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
915 |           }
916 |           token = next();
917 |           continue retry;
918 | 
919 |         default:
920 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
921 |             token = next();
922 |             continue retry;
923 |           } else {
924 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
925 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
926 |             continue retry;
927 |           }
928 |       }
929 | 
930 |       // next token
931 |       token = next();
932 |     }
933 |   }

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 348-364
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
348 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
349 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
350 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
351 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
352 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
353 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
354 |       }
355 |     }
356 | 
357 |     for (Map.Entry<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> symbolEntry : cache.symbols.entrySet()) {
358 |       if (!slots.containsKey(symbolEntry.getKey())) {
359 |         slots.put(symbolEntry.getKey(), symbolEntry.getValue());
360 |       }
361 |     }
362 | 
363 |     return slots;
364 |   }

===== M0617 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(FunctionType,JSDocInfo,Node)

Original range: 761-771
Documentation:
/**
     * If a variable is assigned a function literal in the global scope,
     * make that a declared type (even if there's no doc info).
     * There's only one exception to this rule:
     * if the return type is inferred, and we're in a local
     * scope, we should assume the whole function is inferred.
     */
Method code:
761 |     private boolean shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
762 |         FunctionType type, JSDocInfo info, Node lValue) {
763 |       if (info != null) {
764 |         return true;
765 |       }
766 |       if (lValue != null &&
767 |           NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(lValue, lValue.getParent())) {
768 |         return false;
769 |       }
770 |       return scope.isGlobal() || !type.isReturnTypeInferred();
771 |     }

===== M0630 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 765-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
765 |   @Override
766 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
767 |     FunctionType that =
768 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
769 |     if (that == null) {
770 |       return false;
771 |     }
772 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
773 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
774 |         return this == that;
775 |       }
776 |       return false;
777 |     }
778 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
779 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
780 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
781 |       }
782 |       return false;
783 |     }
784 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
785 |       return false;
786 |     }
787 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
788 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
789 |   }

201 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(

307 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
308 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
309 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
310 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(irNode, node);
311 |     } else {
312 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
313 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
314 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
315 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
316 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
317 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
318 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
319 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
320 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(irNode, node);
321 |       }
322 |     }
323 |   }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line

459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }

===== M0673 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

280 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
281 |     switch (c) {
282 |       case '@':
283 |       case '*':
284 |       case ',':
285 |       case '>':
286 |       case ':':
287 |       case '(':
288 |       case ')':
289 |       case '{':
290 |       case '}':
291 |       case '[':
292 |       case ']':
293 |       case '?':
294 |       case '!':
295 |       case '|':
296 |       case '=':
297 |       case EOF_CHAR:
298 |       case '\n':
299 |         return false;
300 | 
301 |       default:
302 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
303 |     }
304 |   }

===== M0675 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1835-1859
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1835 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1836 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1837 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1838 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1839 |             type.isUnknownType());
1840 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1841 |     if (info != null) {
1842 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1843 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1844 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1845 |         type = infoType;
1846 |       }
1847 | 
1848 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1849 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1850 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1851 |         compiler.report(
1852 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1853 |       }
1854 |     }
1855 | 
1856 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1857 |       n.setJSType(type);
1858 |     }
1859 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1420-1574
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1420 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1421 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1422 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1423 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1424 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1425 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1426 | 
1427 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1428 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1429 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1430 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1431 | 
1432 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1433 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1434 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1435 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1436 |         }
1437 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1438 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1439 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1440 | 
1441 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1442 |         return createOptionalType(
1443 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1444 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1445 | 
1446 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1447 |         return createOptionalType(
1448 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1449 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1450 | 
1451 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1452 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1453 | 
1454 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1455 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1456 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1457 | 
1458 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1459 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1460 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1461 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1462 |           builder.addAlternate(
1463 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1464 |         }
1465 |         return builder.build();
1466 | 
1467 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1468 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1469 | 
1470 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1471 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1472 | 
1473 |       case Token.STRING:
1474 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1475 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1476 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1477 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1478 |         }
1479 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1480 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1481 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1482 |           if (typeList != null &&
1483 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1484 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1485 |             JSType parameterType =
1486 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1487 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1488 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1489 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1490 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1491 |               JSType indexType =
1492 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1493 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1494 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1495 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1496 |             }
1497 |           }
1498 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1499 |         } else {
1500 |           return namedType;
1501 |         }
1502 | 
1503 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1504 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1505 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1506 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1507 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1508 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1509 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1510 |           thisType =
1511 |               ObjectType.cast(
1512 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1513 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1514 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1515 |           if (thisType == null) {
1516 |             reporter.warning(
1517 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1518 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1519 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1520 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1521 |                 sourceName,
1522 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1523 |           }
1524 | 
1525 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1526 |           current = current.getNext();
1527 |         }
1528 | 
1529 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1530 | 
1531 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1532 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1533 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1534 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1535 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1536 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1537 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1538 |               } else {
1539 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1540 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1541 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1542 |               }
1543 |             } else {
1544 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1545 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1546 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1547 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1548 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1549 |                   reporter.warning(
1550 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1551 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1552 |                 }
1553 |               } else {
1554 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1555 |               }
1556 |             }
1557 |           }
1558 |           current = current.getNext();
1559 |         }
1560 | 
1561 |         JSType returnType =
1562 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1563 | 
1564 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1565 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1566 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1567 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1568 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1569 |             .build();
1570 |     }
1571 | 
1572 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1573 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1574 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1332-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1332 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1333 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1334 |       return -1;
1335 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1336 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1337 |     } else {
1338 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

===== M0707 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 183-228
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
183 |   @Override
184 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
185 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
186 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
187 |     // show up in the type registry.
188 |     Scope newScope = null;
189 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
190 |     if (parent == null) {
191 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
192 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
193 | 
194 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
195 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
196 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
197 |     } else {
198 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
199 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
200 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
201 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
202 |     }
203 | 
204 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
205 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
206 | 
207 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
208 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
209 |     // build properties on.
210 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
211 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
212 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
213 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
214 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
215 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
216 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
217 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     if (parent == null) {
223 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
224 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
225 |           delegateCallingConventions);
226 |     }
227 |     return newScope;
228 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-282
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 865-907
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
865 |   @Override
866 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
867 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
868 |       return true;
869 |     }
870 | 
871 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
872 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
873 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
874 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
875 |         return true;
876 |       }
877 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
878 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
879 |         return false;
880 |       }
881 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
882 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
883 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
884 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
885 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
886 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
887 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
888 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
889 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
890 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
891 | 
892 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
893 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
894 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
895 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
896 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
897 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
898 | 
899 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
900 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
901 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
902 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
903 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
904 |     }
905 | 
906 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
907 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

424 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
425 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
426 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
427 |     switch (n.getType()) {
428 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
429 |         // normal type checking
430 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
431 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
432 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
433 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
434 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope

443 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally

445 |         break;

243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:
260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.NEW:
264 |         validateNew(n);
265 |         return;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
268 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
269 |         return;
270 | 
271 |       default:
272 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
273 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
274 |     }
275 |   }

===== M0764 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2297-2313
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2297 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2298 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2299 |     int c;
2300 |     while (true) {
2301 |       c = stream.getChar();
2302 |       if (c == ' ') {
2303 |         continue;
2304 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2305 |         matchedLc = true;
2306 |         break;
2307 |       } else {
2308 |         break;
2309 |       }
2310 |     }
2311 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2312 |     return matchedLc;
2313 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1183 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1184 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1185 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1186 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1187 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1188 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1189 |       } else {
1190 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1191 |       }
1192 |     }
1193 |     return builder.build();
1194 |   }

===== M0787 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1078-1098
Documentation:
/**
   * Suppose X is an object with inferred properties.
   * Suppose also that X is used in a way where it would only type-check
   * correctly if some of those properties are widened.
   * Then we should be polite and automatically widen X's properties for him.
   *
   * For a concrete example, consider:
   * param x {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
   * function f(x) {}
   * f({});
   *
   * If we give the anonymous object an inferred property of (number|undefined),
   * then this code will type-check appropriately.
   */
Method code:
1078 |   private void inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(
1079 |       JSType type, JSType constraint) {
1080 |     ObjectType constraintObj =
1081 |         ObjectType.cast(constraint.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1082 |     if (constraintObj != null && constraintObj.isRecordType()) {
1083 |       ObjectType objType = ObjectType.cast(type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1084 |       if (objType != null) {
1085 |         for (String prop : constraintObj.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
1086 |           JSType propType = constraintObj.getPropertyType(prop);
1087 |           if (!objType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(prop)) {
1088 |             JSType typeToInfer = propType;
1089 |             if (!objType.hasProperty(prop)) {
1090 |               typeToInfer =
1091 |                   getNativeType(VOID_TYPE).getLeastSupertype(propType);
1092 |             }
1093 |             objType.defineInferredProperty(prop, typeToInfer, null);
1094 |           }
1095 |         }
1096 |       }
1097 |     }
1098 |   }

/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
245 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
246 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
247 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
248 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
249 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
250 | 
251 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
252 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
253 |       // if (this.x == null) {
254 |       //   this.initializeX();
255 |       //   this.x.foo();
256 |       // }
257 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
258 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
259 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
260 |       //
261 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
262 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
263 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
264 |         return true;
265 |       }
266 | 
267 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
268 |       return false;
269 |     }
270 |     return true;
271 |   }

===== M0801 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/TokenStream.java
Original range: 314-339
Documentation:

Method code:
314 |     public static boolean isJSIdentifier(String s) {
315 |         int length = s.length();
316 | 
317 |         if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
318 |             return false;
319 | 
320 |         for (int i=1; i<length; i++) {
321 |             char c = s.charAt(i);
322 |             if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(c)) {
323 |                 if (c == '\\') {
324 |                     if (! ((i + 5) < length)
325 |                         && (s.charAt(i + 1) == 'u')
326 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 2), 0)
327 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 3), 0)
328 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 4), 0)
329 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 5), 0)) {
330 |                         return true;
331 |                      }
332 |                 }
333 | 
334 |                 return false;
335 |             }
336 |         }
337 | 
338 |         return true;
339 |     }

===== M0803 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

===== M0806 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 79-140
Documentation:

Method code:
79 |   @Override
80 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType other) {
81 |     if (!(other instanceof ArrowType)) {
82 |       return false;
83 |     }
84 | 
85 |     ArrowType that = (ArrowType) other;
86 | 
87 |     // this.returnType <: that.returnType (covariant)
88 |     if (!this.returnType.isSubtype(that.returnType)) {
89 |       return false;
90 |     }
91 |     // that.paramType[i] <: this.paramType[i] (contravariant)
92 |     // TODO(nicksantos): This is incorrect. It should be invariant.
93 |     // Follow up with closure team on how to fix this without everyone
94 |     // hating on us.
95 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
96 |     Node thatParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
97 |     while (thisParam != null && thatParam != null) {
98 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
99 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
100 |         JSType thatParamType = thatParam.getJSType();
101 |         if (thatParamType == null ||
102 |             !thatParamType.isSubtype(thisParamType)) {
103 |           return false;
104 |         }
105 |       }
106 |       boolean thisIsVarArgs = thisParam.isVarArgs();
107 |       boolean thatIsVarArgs = thatParam.isVarArgs();
108 |       // don't advance if we have variable arguments
109 |       if (!thisIsVarArgs) {
110 |         thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
111 |       }
112 |       if (!thatIsVarArgs) {
113 |         thatParam = thatParam.getNext();
114 |       }
115 |       // both var_args indicates the end
116 |       if (thisIsVarArgs && thatIsVarArgs) {
117 |         thisParam = null;
118 |         thatParam = null;
119 |       }
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     // Right now, the parser's type system doesn't have a good way
123 |     // to model optional arguments.
124 |     //
125 |     // Suppose we have
126 |     // function f(number, number) {}
127 |     // function g(number) {}
128 |     // If the second arg of f is optional, then f is a subtype of g,
129 |     // but g is not a subtype of f.
130 |     // If the second arg of f is required, then g is a subtype of f,
131 |     // but f is not a subtype of g.
132 |     //
133 |     // Until we model optional params, let's just punt on this.
134 |     // If one type has more arguments than the other, we won't check them.
135 |     //
136 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): This is described in Draft 2 of the ES4 spec,
137 |     // Section 3.4.6: Subtyping Function Types. It seems really
138 |     // strange but I haven't thought a lot about the implementation.
139 |     return true;
140 |   }
