===== M0814 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 841-946
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
841 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
842 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
843 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
844 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
845 | 
846 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
847 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
848 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
849 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
850 | 
851 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
852 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
853 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
854 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
855 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
856 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
857 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
858 |         }
859 |       }
860 | 
861 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
862 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
863 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
864 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
865 |             property, rvalue);
866 |         return;
867 |       }
868 | 
869 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
870 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
871 |         checkEnumInitializer(
872 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
873 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
874 |         return;
875 |       }
876 | 
877 |       // object.prototype = ...;
878 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
879 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
880 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
881 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
882 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
883 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
884 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
885 |           }
886 |         } else {
887 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
888 |         }
889 |         return;
890 |       }
891 | 
892 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
893 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
894 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
895 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
896 | 
897 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
898 |           JSType jsType = getJSType(object2);
899 |           if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
900 |             FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
901 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
902 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
903 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
904 |             }
905 |           } else {
906 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
907 |           }
908 |           return;
909 |         }
910 |       }
911 | 
912 |       // object.property = ...;
913 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
914 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
915 |       if (type != null) {
916 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
917 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
918 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
919 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
920 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
921 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
922 |         }
923 |         return;
924 |       }
925 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
926 |       // variable with inferred type case
927 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
928 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
929 |       if (var != null) {
930 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
931 |           return;
932 |         }
933 |       }
934 |     }
935 | 
936 |     // fall through case
937 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
938 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
939 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
940 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
941 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
942 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
943 |     } else {
944 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
945 |     }
946 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1596 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1597 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1598 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1599 |       // top-level unions are allowed

===== M0819 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

/**
   * Gets the greatest subtype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The greatest subtype is the meet (&#8743;) or infimum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8743; Any} = {@code Any}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8743; Object} = {@code Any}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8743; Object} = {@code Number}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
663 |   public JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType that) {
664 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
665 |       // Record types have their own implementation of getGreatestSubtype.
666 |       return that.getGreatestSubtype(this);
667 |     }
668 |     return getGreatestSubtype(this, that);
669 |   }

===== M0836 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 957-1004
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an object literal field definition <code>key : value</code>.
   *
   * If the <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the
   * schema of the object type it is referring to.
   *
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param key the assign node
   */
Method code:
957 |   private void visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal t, Node key, Node objlit) {
958 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Validate get and set function declarations are valid
959 |     // as is the functions can have "extraneous" bits.
960 | 
961 |     // For getter and setter property definitions the
962 |     // rvalue type != the property type.
963 |     Node rvalue = key.getFirstChild();
964 |     JSType rightType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
965 |         key, getJSType(rvalue));
966 |     if (rightType == null) {
967 |       rightType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
968 |     }
969 | 
970 |     Node owner = objlit;
971 | 
972 |     // Validate value is assignable to the key type.
973 | 
974 |     JSType keyType = getJSType(key);
975 |     boolean valid = validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(t, key,
976 |         rightType, keyType,
977 |         owner, NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key));
978 |     if (valid) {
979 |       ensureTyped(t, key, rightType);
980 |     } else {
981 |       ensureTyped(t, key);
982 |     }
983 | 
984 |     // Validate that the key type is assignable to the object property type.
985 |     // This is necessary as the objlit may have been cast to a non-literal
986 |     // object type.
987 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider introducing a CAST node to the AST (or
988 |     // perhaps a parentheses node).
989 | 
990 |     JSType objlitType = getJSType(objlit);
991 |     ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
992 |         objlitType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
993 |     if (type != null) {
994 |       String property = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key);
995 |       if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
996 |           !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
997 |           !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
998 |         validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
999 |             t, key, keyType,
1000 |             type.getPropertyType(property), owner, property);
1001 |       }
1002 |       return;
1003 |     }
1004 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
432 |     @Override
433 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
434 |         Node parent) {
435 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
436 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
437 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
438 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
439 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
443 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
444 |       boolean descend = parent == null || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
445 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
446 | 
447 |       if (descend) {
448 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
449 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
450 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
451 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
452 |                child != null;
453 |                child = child.getNext()) {
454 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
455 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
456 |             }
457 |           }
458 |         }
459 |       }
460 | 
461 |       return descend;
462 |     }

===== M0842 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 907-997
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
907 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
908 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
909 | 
910 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
911 |       return;
912 |     }
913 | 
914 |     int i = 0;
915 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
916 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
917 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
918 |       JSType iParameterType =
919 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
920 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
921 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
922 |         JSType iArgumentType = null;
923 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
924 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
925 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
926 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
927 |             compiler.report(
928 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
929 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
930 |             return;
931 |           }
932 |         }
933 | 
934 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
935 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
936 |         int j = 0;
937 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
938 |           JSType jParameterType =
939 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
940 |           if (jParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
941 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = jParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
942 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
943 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
944 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
945 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
946 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
947 |                 return;
948 |               }
949 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
950 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
951 |               if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
952 |                   jArgumentType.isFunctionType()) {
953 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
954 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
955 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
956 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
957 |                   // using the actual type of T.
958 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = jArgumentType.toMaybeFunctionType();
959 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
960 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
961 |                     // function.
962 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
963 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
964 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
965 |                   }
966 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal does not
967 |                   // reference this.
968 |                   if (!NodeUtil.referencesThis(
969 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
970 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
971 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNREAD_THIS));
972 |                   }
973 |                 } else {
974 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
975 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
976 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
977 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
978 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
979 |                   }
980 |                 }
981 |               }
982 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
983 |               // types of the arguments match.
984 |             }
985 |           }
986 |           j++;
987 |         }
988 | 
989 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
990 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
991 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
992 |           return;
993 |         }
994 |       }
995 |       i++;
996 |     }
997 |   }

===== M0843 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 629-656
Documentation:

Method code:
629 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
630 |     Node lastJump = null;
631 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
632 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
633 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunction(curHandler)) {
634 |         break;
635 |       }
636 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
637 |         if (lastJump == null) {
638 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
639 |         } else {
640 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
641 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
642 |         }
643 |         lastJump = curHandler;
644 |       }
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
648 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     if (lastJump == null) {
652 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
653 |     } else {
654 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
655 |     }
656 |   }

475 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
476 | 
477 |       // type from JSDocInfo
478 |       JSType parameterType = null;
479 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
480 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
481 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
482 |         parameterType =
483 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
484 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
485 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
486 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
487 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
488 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
489 |       } else {
490 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
491 |       }
492 | 
493 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
494 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
495 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
496 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
497 |         }
498 |         foundTemplateType = true;
499 |       }
500 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
501 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
502 |           isOptionalParam,
503 |           isVarArgs);
504 | 
505 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
506 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
507 |       }
508 |     }
509 | 
510 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
511 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
512 |     }
513 | 
514 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
515 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
516 |     }
517 | 
518 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
519 |     return this;
520 |   }

===== M0863 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1723-1756
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1723 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1724 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1725 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1726 |     }
1727 | 
1728 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1729 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1730 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1731 |       skipEOLs();
1732 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1733 |         next();
1734 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1735 |       }
1736 |     }
1737 | 
1738 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName);
1739 | 
1740 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1741 |       next();
1742 |       skipEOLs();
1743 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1744 |       if (memberType != null) {
1745 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1746 | 
1747 |         skipEOLs();
1748 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1749 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1750 |         }
1751 | 
1752 |         next();
1753 |       }
1754 |     }
1755 |     return typeNameNode;
1756 |   }

===== M0869 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getGreatestSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 675-709
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common
   * getGreatestSubtype implementations.
   */
Method code:
675 |   static JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
676 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
677 |       return thisType;
678 |     } else if (thisType.isUnknownType() || thatType.isUnknownType()) {
679 |       // The greatest subtype with any unknown type is the universal
680 |       // unknown type, unless the two types are equal.
681 |       return thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType) ? thisType :
682 |           thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
683 |     } else if (thisType.isSubtype(thatType)) {
684 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thisType);
685 |     } else if (thatType.isSubtype(thisType)) {
686 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thatType);
687 |     } else if (thisType.isUnionType()) {
688 |       return thisType.toMaybeUnionType().meet(thatType);
689 |     } else if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
690 |       return thatType.toMaybeUnionType().meet(thisType);
691 |     }
692 | 
693 |     if (thisType.isEnumElementType()) {
694 |       JSType inf = thisType.toMaybeEnumElementType().meet(thatType);
695 |       if (inf != null) {
696 |         return inf;
697 |       }
698 |     } else if (thatType.isEnumElementType()) {
699 |       JSType inf = thatType.toMaybeEnumElementType().meet(thisType);
700 |       if (inf != null) {
701 |         return inf;
702 |       }
703 |     }
704 | 
705 |     if (thisType.isObject() && thatType.isObject()) {
706 |       return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_OBJECT_TYPE);
707 |     }
708 |     return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
709 |   }

===== M0871 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1645-1684
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1645 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1646 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1647 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1648 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1649 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1650 |       // {?} - right curly
1651 |       // {?=} - equals
1652 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1653 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1654 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1655 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1656 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1657 |       token = next();
1658 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1659 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1660 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1661 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1662 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1663 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1664 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1665 |       }
1666 | 
1667 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1668 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1669 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1670 |     } else {
1671 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1672 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1673 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1674 |           next();
1675 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1676 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1677 |           next();
1678 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1679 |         }
1680 |       }
1681 | 
1682 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1683 |     }
1684 |   }

===== M0885 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1219-1248
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1219 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1220 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1221 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1222 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1223 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1224 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1225 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1226 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1227 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1228 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1229 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1230 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1231 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1232 |       return false;
1233 |     }
1234 | 
1235 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1236 |     if (type == null) {
1237 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1238 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1239 |       if (var != null) {
1240 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1241 |         if (varType != null) {
1242 |           type = varType;
1243 |         }
1244 |       }
1245 |     }
1246 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1247 |     return true;
1248 |   }

/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
893 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
894 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
895 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
896 |     }
897 | 
898 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
899 | 
900 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
901 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
902 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
903 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
904 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
905 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
906 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
907 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
908 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
909 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
910 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
911 |         } else {
912 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
913 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));

915 |       }
916 |     }
917 |   }

===== M0895 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1722-1741
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1722 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1723 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1724 |       case Token.IF:
1725 |       case Token.WHILE:
1726 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1727 |       case Token.DO:
1728 |         return n.getLastChild();
1729 |       case Token.FOR:
1730 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1731 |           case 3:
1732 |             return null;
1733 |           case 4:
1734 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1735 |         }
1736 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1737 |       case Token.CASE:
1738 |         return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1741 |   }

===== M0897 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 123-263
Documentation:

Method code:
123 |   @Override
124 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
125 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
126 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
127 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
128 |     switch (operatorToken) {
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.NE:
131 |       case Token.SHEQ:
132 |       case Token.SHNE:
133 |       case Token.CASE:
134 |         Node left;
135 |         Node right;
136 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
137 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
138 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
139 |         } else {
140 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
141 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
145 |         Node stringNode = null;
146 |         if (left.getType() == Token.TYPEOF && right.getType() == Token.STRING) {
147 |           typeOfNode = left;
148 |           stringNode = right;
149 |         } else if (right.getType() == Token.TYPEOF &&
150 |                    left.getType() == Token.STRING) {
151 |           typeOfNode = right;
152 |           stringNode = left;
153 |         }
154 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
155 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
156 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
157 |           if (operandType != null) {
158 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
159 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
160 |             if (!outcome) {
161 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
162 |             }
163 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
164 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
165 |           }
166 |         }
167 |     }
168 |     switch (operatorToken) {
169 |       case Token.AND:
170 |         if (outcome) {
171 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
172 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
173 |         } else {
174 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
175 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
176 |         }
177 | 
178 |       case Token.OR:
179 |         if (!outcome) {
180 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
181 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
182 |         } else {
183 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
184 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
185 |         }
186 | 
187 |       case Token.EQ:
188 |         if (outcome) {
189 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
190 |         } else {
191 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
192 |         }
193 | 
194 |       case Token.NE:
195 |         if (outcome) {
196 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
197 |         } else {
198 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |       case Token.SHEQ:
202 |         if (outcome) {
203 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
204 |         } else {
205 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
206 |         }
207 | 
208 |       case Token.SHNE:
209 |         if (outcome) {
210 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
211 |         } else {
212 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
213 |         }
214 | 
215 |       case Token.NAME:
216 |       case Token.GETPROP:
217 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
218 | 
219 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
220 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
221 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
222 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
223 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
224 |             outcome);
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NOT:
227 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
228 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
229 | 
230 |       case Token.LE:
231 |       case Token.LT:
232 |       case Token.GE:
233 |       case Token.GT:
234 |         if (outcome) {
235 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, INEQ);
236 |         }
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
240 |         return caseInstanceOf(
241 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
242 |             outcome);
243 | 
244 |       case Token.IN:
245 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.STRING) {
246 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
247 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
248 |         }
249 |         break;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.CASE:
252 |         Node left =
253 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
254 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
255 |         if (outcome) {
256 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
257 |         } else {
258 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
259 |         }
260 |     }
261 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
262 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
263 |   }

===== M0900 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1510-1540
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1510 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1511 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1512 |         Node n = stub.node;
1513 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1514 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1515 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1516 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1517 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1518 | 
1519 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1520 |           continue;
1521 |         }
1522 | 
1523 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1524 |         // in the type registry.
1525 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1526 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1527 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1528 | 
1529 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1530 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1531 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1532 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1533 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1534 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1535 |         } else {
1536 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1537 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1538 |         }
1539 |       }
1540 |     }

/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
313 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
314 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
315 |     if (functionBlock == null || functionBlock.isFromExterns()) {
316 |       return this;
317 |     }
318 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
319 |     if (returnType == null) {
320 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
321 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
322 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
323 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
324 |         int cType = current.getType();
325 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null) {
326 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
327 |           break;
328 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
329 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
330 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
331 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
332 |             worklist.add(child);
333 |           }
334 |         }
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
338 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
339 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
340 |       }
341 |     }
342 |     return this;
343 |   }

/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1183 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1184 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1185 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1186 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1187 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1188 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1189 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1190 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1191 |       } else if (info != null) {
1192 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1193 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1194 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1195 |           } else {
1196 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1197 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1198 |           }
1199 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1200 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1201 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1202 |         } else {
1203 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1204 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1205 |             JSType knownType = null;
1206 |             if (rValue != null) {
1207 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1208 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1209 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1210 |               } else if (rValue.getType() == Token.OR) {
1211 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1212 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1213 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1214 |                 // reasons.
1215 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1216 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1217 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.getType() == Token.NAME
1218 |                     && lValue.getType() == Token.NAME
1219 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1220 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1221 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1222 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1223 |                 }
1224 |               }
1225 |             }
1226 |           }
1227 |         }
1228 |       }
1229 | 
1230 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1231 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

999 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1000 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
1001 |     scope = traverse(constructor, scope);
1002 | 
1003 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
1004 |     JSType type = null;
1005 |     if (constructorType != null) {
1006 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1007 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
1008 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1009 |       } else {
1010 |         FunctionType ct = constructorType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1011 |         if (ct == null && constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {

1025 |       scope = traverse(arg, scope);
1026 |     }
1027 |     return scope;
1028 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0942 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
161 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0945 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0964 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 493-539
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
493 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
494 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
495 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
496 |     switch (left.getType()) {
497 |       case Token.NAME:
498 |         String varName = left.getString();
499 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
500 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
501 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
502 |         }
503 | 
504 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
505 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
506 |         // This has two purposes:
507 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
508 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
509 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
510 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
511 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
512 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
513 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
514 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
515 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
516 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
517 |         }
518 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
519 |             resultType : null);
520 | 
521 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
522 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
523 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
524 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
525 |         }
526 |         break;
527 |       case Token.GETPROP:
528 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
529 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
530 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
531 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
532 |               resultType);
533 |         }
534 | 
535 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
536 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
537 |         break;
538 |     }
539 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 786-800
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }

===== M0972 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1690-1716
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1690 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1691 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1692 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1693 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1694 |       skipEOLs();
1695 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1696 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1697 |       skipEOLs();
1698 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1699 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1700 |       skipEOLs();
1701 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1702 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1703 |       String string = stream.getString();
1704 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1705 |         skipEOLs();
1706 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1707 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1708 |         return newStringNode(string);
1709 |       } else {
1710 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1711 |       }
1712 |     }
1713 | 
1714 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1715 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1716 |   }

/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1594 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1595 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1596 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1597 |     }
1598 | 
1599 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1600 |     Node n, n2;
1601 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1602 |          res == null && n != null;
1603 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1604 |       if (node2 == null) {
1605 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1606 |       }
1607 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1608 |       if (res != null) {
1609 |         return res;
1610 |       }
1611 |     }
1612 |     return res;
1613 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.

   */
Method code:
378 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
379 |       JSType leftType, String msg) {
380 |     if (!rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
381 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
382 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
383 |         // the line numbers of both types.

===== M1000 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 464-512
Documentation:

Method code:
464 |     @Override
465 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
466 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
467 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
468 | 
469 |       switch (n.getType()) {
470 |         case Token.CALL:
471 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
472 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
473 |           break;
474 | 
475 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
476 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
477 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
478 |           }
479 | 
480 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
481 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
482 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
483 |           }
484 |           break;
485 | 
486 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
487 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
488 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
489 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
490 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
491 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
492 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
493 |           }
494 |           break;
495 | 
496 |         case Token.CATCH:
497 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
498 |           break;
499 | 
500 |         case Token.VAR:
501 |           defineVar(n, parent);
502 |           break;
503 | 
504 |         case Token.GETPROP:
505 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
506 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
507 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
508 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
509 |           }
510 |           break;
511 |       }
512 |     }

===== M1007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-88
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         }
70 | 
71 |         if (info != null) {
72 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
73 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
74 | 
75 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
76 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
77 |             if (guard != null) {
78 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
79 |               if (newLevel != null) {
80 |                 return newLevel;
81 |               }
82 |             }
83 |           }
84 |         }
85 |       }
86 |     }
87 |     return null;
88 |   }

677 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));

===== M1017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 1012-1058
Documentation:

Method code:
1012 |   @Override
1013 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1014 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this);
1015 | 
1016 |     call = (ArrowType) safeResolve(call, t, scope);
1017 |     prototype = (PrototypeObjectType) safeResolve(prototype, t, scope);
1018 | 
1019 |     // Warning about typeOfThis if it doesn't resolve to an ObjectType
1020 |     // is handled further upstream.
1021 |     //
1022 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
1023 |     //
1024 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
1025 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
1026 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
1027 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
1028 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
1029 |     // the name for them.

1032 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1033 |     }
1034 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
1035 |       typeOfThis = (ObjectType) maybeTypeOfThis;
1036 |     }
1037 | 
1038 |     boolean changed = false;
1039 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
1040 |         ImmutableList.builder();
1041 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
1042 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
1043 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1044 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1045 |     }
1046 |     if (changed) {
1047 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
1048 |     }
1049 | 
1050 |     if (subTypes != null) {
1051 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
1052 |         subTypes.set(
1053 |             i, JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope)));
1054 |       }
1055 |     }
1056 | 
1057 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
1058 |   }

301 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
302 |     switch (n.getType()) {
303 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
304 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.NAME:
308 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
309 |         break;
310 | 
311 |       case Token.GETPROP:
312 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
313 |         break;
314 | 
315 |       case Token.AND:
316 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
317 |             .createChildFlowScope();
318 |         break;
319 | 
320 |       case Token.OR:
321 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
322 |             .createChildFlowScope();
323 |         break;
324 | 
325 |       case Token.HOOK:
326 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
327 |         break;
328 | 
329 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
330 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
331 |         break;
332 | 
333 |       case Token.CALL:
334 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
335 |         break;
336 | 
337 |       case Token.NEW:
338 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
339 |         break;
340 | 
341 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
342 |       case Token.ADD:
343 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
344 |         break;
345 | 
346 |       case Token.POS:
347 |       case Token.NEG:
348 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
349 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
353 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.THIS:
357 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
358 |         break;
359 | 
360 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
361 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
362 |       case Token.LSH:
363 |       case Token.RSH:
364 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
365 |       case Token.URSH:
366 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
367 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
373 |       case Token.DIV:
374 |       case Token.MOD:
375 |       case Token.BITAND:
376 |       case Token.BITXOR:
377 |       case Token.BITOR:
378 |       case Token.MUL:
379 |       case Token.SUB:
380 |       case Token.DEC:
381 |       case Token.INC:
382 |       case Token.BITNOT:
383 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
384 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
385 |         break;
386 | 
387 |       case Token.LP:
388 |       case Token.GET_REF:
389 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
390 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
391 |         break;
392 | 
393 |       case Token.COMMA:
394 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
395 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
396 |         break;
397 | 
398 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
399 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
400 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
401 |         break;
402 | 
403 |       case Token.DELPROP:
404 |       case Token.LT:
405 |       case Token.LE:
406 |       case Token.GT:
407 |       case Token.GE:
408 |       case Token.NOT:
409 |       case Token.EQ:
410 |       case Token.NE:
411 |       case Token.SHEQ:
412 |       case Token.SHNE:
413 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
414 |       case Token.IN:
415 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
416 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
417 |         break;
418 | 
419 |       case Token.GETELEM:
420 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
421 |         break;
422 | 
423 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
424 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
425 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
426 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
427 |         }
428 |         break;
429 | 
430 |       case Token.SWITCH:
431 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
432 |         break;
433 | 
434 |       case Token.VAR:
435 |       case Token.RETURN:
436 |       case Token.THROW:
437 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.CATCH:
441 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
442 |         break;
443 |     }
444 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
445 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
446 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
447 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
448 | 
449 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
450 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
451 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
452 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
453 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
454 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
455 |         }
456 | 
457 |         n.setJSType(castType);
458 |       }
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     return scope;
462 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2923-2938
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2923 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2924 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2925 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2926 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2927 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2928 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2929 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2930 |         // on ASSIGNs
2931 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2932 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2933 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2934 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2935 |       }
2936 |     }
2937 |     return fnInfo;
2938 |   }

===== M1026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

1013 |      * Returns a node comparator based on the pre-order traversal of the AST.
1014 |      * @param isForward x 'before' y in the pre-order traversal implies

1024 |             return getPosition(n1) - getPosition(n2);
1025 |           }
1026 |         };
1027 |       } else {

1029 |           @Override
1030 |           public int compare(
1031 |               DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n1, DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n2) {
1032 |             return getPosition(n2) - getPosition(n1);
1033 |           }

/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
806 |   @Override
807 |   public String toString() {
808 |     if (this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
809 |       return "Function";
810 |     }
811 | 
812 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
813 |     b.append("function (");
814 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
815 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !typeOfThis.isUnknownType();
816 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
817 |       if (isConstructor()) {
818 |         b.append("new:");
819 |       } else {
820 |         b.append("this:");
821 |       }
822 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toString());
823 |     }
824 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
825 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
826 |         b.append(", ");
827 |       }
828 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
829 |       if (p.isVarArgs()) {
830 |         appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
831 |       } else {
832 |         b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
833 |       }
834 |       p = p.getNext();
835 |       while (p != null) {
836 |         b.append(", ");
837 |         if (p.isVarArgs()) {
838 |           appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
839 |         } else {
840 |           b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
841 |         }
842 |         p = p.getNext();
843 |       }
844 |     }
845 |     b.append("): ");
846 |     b.append(call.returnType);
847 |     return b.toString();
848 |   }

===== M1049 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 67-122
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
67 |   @Override
68 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
69 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
70 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
71 |     if (type != null) {
72 |       Node subclass = null;
73 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
74 | 
75 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
76 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
77 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
78 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
79 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
80 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
81 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
83 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP;
84 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
85 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
86 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
87 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
88 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
89 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
90 |       } else {
91 |         return null;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
95 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
96 |         // inheritance.
97 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
98 |           return null;
99 |         }
100 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
101 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
102 |             return null;
103 |           }
104 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
105 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
106 |         }
107 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
108 |       }
109 | 
110 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
111 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
112 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
113 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
114 |       if (subclass != null &&
115 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
116 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
117 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
118 |       }
119 |     }
120 | 
121 |     return null;
122 |   }

===== M1067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType)

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
357 |   public boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype) {
358 |     if (prototype == null) {
359 |       return false;
360 |     }
361 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
362 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
363 |       return false;
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
367 | 
368 |     this.prototype = prototype;
369 |     this.prototypeSlot = new SimpleSlot("prototype", prototype, true);
370 |     this.prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
371 | 
372 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
373 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
374 | 
375 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
376 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
377 |       if (superClass != null) {
378 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
379 |       }
380 | 
381 |       if (isInterface()) {
382 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
383 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
384 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
385 |           }
386 |         }
387 |       }
388 |     }
389 | 
390 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
391 |       clearCachedValues();
392 |     }
393 | 
394 |     return true;
395 |   }

902 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
903 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
904 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
905 |       // on a superclass.
906 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1097 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {

1101 | 
1102 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1103 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1104 | 
1105 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
1106 |         true);
1107 |     return typeNode;
1108 |   }
