===== M1086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 441-489
Documentation:

Method code:
441 |   @Override
442 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
443 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
444 |       return true;
445 |     }
446 | 
447 |     // Union types
448 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
449 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
450 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
451 |       return false;
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // record types
455 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
456 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     // Interfaces
460 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
461 |     // to the interface instance.
462 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
463 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
464 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
465 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
466 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
467 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
468 |           return true;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 |     }
472 | 
473 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
474 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
475 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
476 |           return true;
477 |         }
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 | 
481 |     // other prototype based objects
482 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
483 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
484 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
485 |       // to avoid guessing.
486 |       return true;
487 |     }
488 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
489 |   }

569 |           type = lendsVar.getType();
570 |           if (type == null) {
571 |             type = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
572 |           }
573 |           if (!type.isSubtype(typeRegistry.getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
574 |             compiler.report(
575 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, objectLit, LENDS_ON_NON_OBJECT,
576 |                     lendsName, type.toString()));
577 |             type = null;
578 |           } else {
579 |             objectLit.setJSType(type);
580 |           }
581 |         }
582 |       }
583 | 
584 |       info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(objectLit);
585 |       Node lValue = NodeUtil.getBestLValue(objectLit);
586 |       String lValueName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(lValue);
587 |       boolean createdEnumType = false;
588 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
589 |         type = createEnumTypeFromNodes(objectLit, lValueName, info, lValue);
590 |         createdEnumType = true;
591 |       }
592 | 
593 |       if (type == null) {
594 |         type = typeRegistry.createAnonymousObjectType();
595 |       }
596 | 
597 |       setDeferredType(objectLit, type);
598 | 
599 |       // If this is an enum, the properties were already taken care of above.
600 |       if (!createdEnumType) {
601 |         processObjectLitProperties(
602 |             t, objectLit, ObjectType.cast(objectLit.getJSType()));
603 |       }
604 |     }

===== M1105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 795-897
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
795 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
796 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
797 |         @Nullable String name,
798 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
799 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
800 | 
801 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
802 | 
803 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
804 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
805 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
806 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
807 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
808 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
809 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
810 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
811 |             functionType = aliasedType;
812 | 
813 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
814 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
815 |             }
816 |           }
817 |         }
818 |       }
819 | 
820 |       if (functionType == null) {
821 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
822 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
823 |             rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
824 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
825 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
826 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
827 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
828 | 
829 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
830 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
831 | 
832 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
833 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
834 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
835 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
836 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
837 |           }
838 |         }
839 | 
840 |         if (functionType == null) {
841 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
842 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
843 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
844 |           Var ownerVar = null;
845 |           String propName = null;
846 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
847 |           if (ownerName != null) {
848 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
849 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
850 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
851 |             }
852 |             if (name != null) {
853 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
854 |             }
855 |           }
856 | 
857 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
858 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
859 |             overriddenPropType =
860 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
861 |           }
862 | 
863 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
864 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
865 |                   scope)
866 |               .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
867 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
868 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
869 |               .inferReturnType(info)
870 |               .inferInheritance(info);
871 | 
872 | 
873 |           // Infer the context type.
874 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
875 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
876 |             builder.inferThisType(
877 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
878 |             searchedForThisType = true;
879 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
880 |             builder.inferThisType(info, ownerNode.getJSType());
881 |             searchedForThisType = true;
882 |           }
883 | 
884 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
885 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
886 |           }
887 | 
888 |           functionType = builder
889 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
890 |               .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
891 |               .buildAndRegister();
892 |         }
893 |       }
894 | 
895 |       // all done
896 |       return functionType;
897 |     }

===== M1122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-735
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(
100 |           childCount == 2,
101 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
102 |           opstr, childCount);
103 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
104 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
105 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
106 | 
107 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
108 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
109 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
110 | 
111 |       // Handle associativity.
112 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
113 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
114 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
115 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
118 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
119 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
120 |       } else {
121 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
122 |       }
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
127 | 
128 |     switch (type) {
129 |       case Token.TRY: {
130 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
131 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
132 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
133 | 
134 |         add("try");
135 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 | 
137 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
138 |         // isn't a catch block
139 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
140 |         if (catchblock != null) {
141 |           add(catchblock);
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         if (childCount == 3) {
145 |           add("finally");
146 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
147 |         }
148 |         break;
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       case Token.CATCH:
152 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
153 |         add("catch(");
154 |         add(first);
155 |         add(")");
156 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
157 |         break;
158 | 
159 |       case Token.THROW:
160 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
161 |         add("throw");
162 |         add(first);
163 | 
164 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
165 |         // parse this.
166 |         cc.endStatement(true);
167 |         break;
168 | 
169 |       case Token.RETURN:
170 |         add("return");
171 |         if (childCount == 1) {
172 |           add(first);
173 |         } else {
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
175 |         }
176 |         cc.endStatement();
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.VAR:
180 |         if (first != null) {
181 |           add("var ");
182 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
187 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
188 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         break;
190 | 
191 |       case Token.NAME:
192 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |         } else {
195 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
196 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
197 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
198 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
199 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
200 |           } else {
201 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
202 |             // precedence.
203 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
204 |           }
205 |         }
206 |         break;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |         add("[");
210 |         addArrayList(first);
211 |         add("]");
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.LP:
215 |         add("(");
216 |         addList(first);
217 |         add(")");
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.COMMA:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
222 |         addList(first, false, context);
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.NUMBER:
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
227 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
231 |       case Token.VOID:
232 |       case Token.NOT:
233 |       case Token.BITNOT:
234 |       case Token.POS: {
235 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
237 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
238 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
239 |         break;
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       case Token.NEG: {
243 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
244 | 
245 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
246 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
247 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
248 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
249 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
250 |         } else {
251 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
252 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         break;
256 |       }
257 | 
258 |       case Token.HOOK: {
259 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
260 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
261 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
262 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
263 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
264 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
265 |         addExpr(last, 1);
266 |         break;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       case Token.REGEXP:
270 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
271 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
272 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
276 | 
277 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
278 |         if (childCount == 2) {
279 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
280 |         } else {
281 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
282 |           add(regexp);
283 |         }
284 |         break;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.GET_REF:
287 |         add(first);
288 |         break;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
292 |         add(first);
293 |         add(".");
294 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
302 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
303 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
304 |           add("(");
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         add("function");
308 |         add(first);
309 | 
310 |         add(first.getNext());
311 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
312 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
313 | 
314 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
315 |           add(")");
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.GET:
320 |       case Token.SET:
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
323 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
324 | 
325 |         // Get methods are unnamed
326 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
327 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
328 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
330 |           add("get ");
331 |         } else {
332 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
333 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
334 |           add("set ");
335 |         }
336 | 
337 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
338 |         String name = n.getString();
339 |         Node fn = first;
340 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
341 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
342 | 
343 |         // Add the property name.
344 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
345 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
346 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
347 |             // unicode escaped.
348 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
349 |           add(name);
350 |         } else {
351 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
352 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
353 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
354 |             cc.addNumber(d);
355 |           } else {
356 |             addJsString(n.getString());
357 |           }
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         add(parameters);
361 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
362 |         break;
363 | 
364 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
365 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
366 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
367 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
368 |         }
369 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
370 |         if (preserveBlock) {
371 |           cc.beginBlock();
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
375 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
376 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
377 |                 !preserveBlock &&
378 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
379 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
380 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
381 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
382 | 
383 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
385 |             cc.endStatement();
386 |           }
387 | 
388 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
389 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
390 |           }
391 | 
392 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
393 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
394 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
395 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |         if (preserveBlock) {
399 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
400 |         }
401 |         break;
402 |       }
403 | 
404 |       case Token.FOR:
405 |         if (childCount == 4) {
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
408 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
409 |           } else {
410 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
411 |           }
412 |           add(";");
413 |           add(first.getNext());
414 |           add(";");
415 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
416 |           add(")");
417 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
418 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
419 |         } else {
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
421 |           add("for(");
422 |           add(first);
423 |           add("in");
424 |           add(first.getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.DO:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("do");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(last);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         cc.endStatement();
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.WHILE:
442 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(first);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
447 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.EMPTY:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(
456 |             childCount == 2,
457 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
458 |         Preconditions.checkState(
459 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
460 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
461 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
462 |         if (needsParens) {
463 |           add("(");
464 |         }
465 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
466 |         if (needsParens) {
467 |           add(")");
468 |         }
469 |         add(".");
470 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
471 |         break;
472 |       }
473 | 
474 |       case Token.GETELEM:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(
476 |             childCount == 2,
477 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
478 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
479 |         add("[");
480 |         add(first.getNext());
481 |         add("]");
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.WITH:
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         add("with(");
487 |         add(first);
488 |         add(")");
489 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
490 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.INC:
494 |       case Token.DEC: {
495 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
496 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
497 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
498 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
499 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
500 |         if (postProp != 0) {
501 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
502 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
503 |         } else {
504 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
505 |           add(first);
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       case Token.CALL:
511 |         // We have two special cases here:
512 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
513 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
515 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
516 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
517 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
518 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
519 |         // that must be preserved.
520 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
521 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
522 |           add("(0,");
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
524 |           add(")");
525 |         } else {
526 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
527 |         }
528 |         add("(");
529 |         addList(first.getNext());
530 |         add(")");
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.IF:
534 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
535 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
536 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
537 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
538 |           cc.beginBlock();
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         add("if(");
542 |         add(first);
543 |         add(")");
544 | 
545 |         if (hasElse) {
546 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
547 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
548 |           add("else");
549 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
550 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
551 |         } else {
552 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
557 |           cc.endBlock();
558 |         }
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NULL:
562 |       case Token.THIS:
563 |       case Token.FALSE:
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
602 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
603 | 
604 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
605 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
606 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
607 |         cc.endStatement();
608 |         break;
609 | 
610 |       case Token.NEW:
611 |         add("new ");
612 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
613 | 
614 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
615 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
616 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
617 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 761-798
Documentation:

Method code:
761 |     @Override
762 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
763 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
764 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
765 |       }
766 | 
767 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
768 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
769 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
770 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
771 |             reportGetter(el);
772 |             continue;
773 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
774 |             reportSetter(el);
775 |             continue;
776 |           }
777 |         }
778 | 
779 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
780 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
781 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
782 |           key.setType(Token.GET);
783 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
784 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
785 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
786 |           }
787 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
788 |           key.setType(Token.SET);
789 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
790 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
791 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
792 |           }
793 |         }
794 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
795 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
796 |       }
797 |       return node;
798 |     }

===== M1124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 670-706
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
670 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
671 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
672 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
673 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
674 | 
675 |     if (existingType != null) {
676 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
677 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
678 |         FunctionType existingFn =
679 |             isInstanceObject ?
680 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
681 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
682 | 
683 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
684 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
685 |         }
686 | 
687 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
688 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
689 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
690 |         }
691 | 
692 |         return existingFn;
693 |       } else {
694 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
695 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
696 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
697 |       }
698 |     }
699 | 
700 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
701 | 
702 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
703 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
704 |     }
705 |     return fnType;
706 |   }

/**
   * Two named types are equivalent if they are the same {@code
   * ObjectType} object.  This is complicated by the fact that isEquivalent
   * is sometimes called before we have a chance to resolve the type
   * names.
   *
   * @return {@code true} iff {@code that} == {@code this} or {@code that}
   *         is a {@link NamedType} whose reference is the same as ours,
   *         or {@code that} is the type we reference.
   */
Method code:
185 |   @Override
186 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {
187 |     if (this == that) {
188 |       return true;
189 |     }
190 | 
191 |     ObjectType objType = ObjectType.cast(that);
192 |     if (objType != null) {
193 |       return objType.isNominalType() &&
194 |           reference.equals(objType.getReferenceName());
195 |     }
196 |     return false;
197 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken,int,int,boolean,boolean)

Original range: 1123-1145
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1123 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
1124 |                                       int startCharno,
1125 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
1126 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1127 |     Node typeNode = null;
1128 | 
1129 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1130 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
1131 |     } else {

   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.

Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M1136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 187-299
Documentation:

Method code:
187 |   @Override
188 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
189 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
190 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
191 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
192 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
193 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
194 |     // worthwhile.
195 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
196 |     Node condition = null;
197 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
198 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
199 | 
200 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
201 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
202 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
203 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
204 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
205 | 
206 |       switch (branch) {
207 |         case ON_TRUE:
208 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
209 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
210 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
211 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
212 | 
213 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
214 | 
215 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
216 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
217 |             }
218 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
219 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
220 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
221 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
222 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
223 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
224 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
225 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
226 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
227 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
228 |                 }
229 |               }
230 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
231 |             }
232 |             newScope = informed;
233 |             break;
234 |           }
235 | 
236 |           // FALL THROUGH
237 | 
238 |         case ON_FALSE:
239 |           if (condition == null) {
240 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
241 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
242 |               condition = source;
243 | 
244 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
245 |               // of the loop.
246 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
247 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
248 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
249 |               }
250 |             }
251 |           }
252 | 
253 |           if (condition != null) {
254 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
255 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
256 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
257 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
258 |               // scope on false.
259 |               //
260 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
261 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
262 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
263 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
264 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
265 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
266 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
267 | 
268 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
269 |               // of the loop.
270 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
271 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
272 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
273 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
274 |               }
275 |               newScope =
276 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
277 |                       condition,
278 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
279 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
280 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
281 |             } else {
282 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
283 |               // of the loop.
284 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
285 |                 conditionFlowScope =
286 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
287 |               }
288 |               newScope =
289 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
290 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
291 |             }
292 |           }
293 |           break;
294 |       }
295 | 
296 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
297 |     }
298 |     return result;
299 |   }

/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
228 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
229 |     this.options = options;
230 |     if (errorManager == null) {
231 |       if (outStream == null) {
232 |         setErrorManager(
233 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
234 |       } else {
235 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
236 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
237 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
238 |         setErrorManager(printer);
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
243 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = true;
245 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
246 |       options.checkTypes = false;
247 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
248 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
249 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
250 |       // parser type warnings.
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
253 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
254 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
258 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
259 |       options.setWarningLevel(
260 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
261 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
267 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
271 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
272 |     guards.add(
273 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
274 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
275 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
276 | 
277 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
278 | 
279 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
280 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
281 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
282 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
283 |     // error off.
284 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
285 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
286 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
287 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
288 |     }
289 | 
290 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
291 |   }

Method code:
647 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
648 |     if (child == first) {
649 |       return null;
650 |     }
651 |     Node n = first;
652 |     while (n.next != child) {
653 |       n = n.next;
654 |       if (n == null) {
655 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
656 |       }
657 |     }
658 |     return n;
659 |   }

571 |   public Scope getScope() {
572 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
573 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
574 |       return scope;
575 |     }

580 |       scopes.push(scope);
581 |     }
582 |     scopeRoots.clear();
583 | 
584 |     return scope;
